The present verse is the one that stands apart from the statements, since the love of God for the certain people is the love that is tied directly to their love of Him, and the tying is the act that the verse does in the form of the phrase, which is the form of the two loves held together in the single line.

**The Mystic Reading of the Love**

A number of the commentators found the idea of a bond of love between the human being and God difficult to hold, and the difficulty is the one that the inequality of the two gives. Some of the commentators took the ones who love Him as the ones who obey God and seek to please Him, and the ones whom He loves as the ones whom He rewards for the obedience, and the taking is the one that the theology of the obedience gives. The taking is not the one that the order of the verse supports, since the obedience, which is the love of the servants toward God, ought to come before the reward of God, whereas the verse points the other way, in the form in which the love of God for the people is the thing that comes first. The writers of the inner way joined the verse to the idea that the turning of the human heart toward God is the result of God having turned toward the people before, and the turning is the act that the idea states, and another of the ideas states that the love of God for the human beings is what brings forth the love of the human beings for God. One of the writers of the renewal of the sciences takes the verse in the sense that the love of the human being for God in this verse cannot be reduced to the gloss of the obedience, which by its nature comes before the reward of God. Others of the commentators spoke against the interpreters who set up a bond of love that is of the essence between the human being and God, in the strongest of the terms, on the ground of the inequality of the two, and the writers of the inner way answered in the several ways. One of the writers holds that the love of God for anything, the humanity included, is the love of God for Himself, since God is the infinite and nothing is outside of His being, and the love of the human being for God is nothing but the deliberate unveiling of God of Himself in the heart of the one who seeks the nearness, and the seeking is made possible only by the determination of God. The poet of the inner way quotes the verse in the major work and holds that the love is the attribute whose own is God alone, and the human beings share in it in the way of the derivative. One of the writers states that the love of God for certain of His creatures is the love that stands closer than the mercy that God carries toward all of His creatures, and the love is the more exclusive and the more ennobling bond that God has with certain of His servants. The writer sets the love of the servant for God apart from the other forms of the devotion and the obedience, and states that the love is the subtle state that lets the servant take pleasure in the Presence of God, and the state leads to the complete vanishing of the separate existence of the lover in the remembrance of God, and the writer describes the state as the intoxication that the one does not come out of and the sickness that has no remedy and for which no remedy is asked.

**The Two Faces of the Ones Brought**

The verse describes the people whom God brings in the two faces, and the two faces are the face of the lowliness before the believers and the face of the firmness before the disbelievers. The lowliness is the lowliness that the word carries not only in the sense of the humbling but also in the sense of the sympathy and the mercy and the tenderness, and the firmness is the harshness that shows the might in the act toward the disbelievers. The verse of the description of the followers of the prophet sets the parallel mark in the form of the statement that the ones who are with the prophet are the ones who are firm toward the disbelievers and tender toward one another, and the parallel is the act that the Book does in the form of the description of the same state. The struggling in the way of God, which the verse states for the people, is the struggling that many of the commentators join to the fight of the early community against the tribes that had left, and the struggling is the one that the writer of the discourse on the love takes in the purely spiritual sense, which is the effort to keep the perfect obedience to the commands of God, to loose the one from the attachments of the world, and to stand as the witness to God at every moment. The phrase that closes the description, which states that this is the favor of God, is the phrase that points at the presence of the meritorious qualities of the believers that the verse names, and one of the commentators takes the phrase as the proof that every good thing, the righteous acts of the human beings included, comes in the end from God, and the other takes the phrase as the grace of God by which He leads the people toward the belief and the good acts, and the two readings together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the close of the description.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:55
> Your only guardians are Allah, His Messenger, and fellow believers— who establish prayer and pay alms-tax with humility.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Order of the Guardians in the Address**

The sūrah carries on the account of the guardians from the verse of the prohibition, and the carrying on is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the second verse of the account. The verse of the prohibition forbids the taking of the two communities as the guardians, and this verse states the taking that is the taking the believers are to do in its place, and the taking is the taking of God alone, and of His messenger, and of the ones who believe. The verse puts God at the head of the order of the guardians, and the head is the place that the verse gives to God in the form of the address, since God is the first of the guardians in the order that the sūrah keeps, and one of the Names of God is the name of the guardian of the one who seeks the guarding. The taking of the messenger and of the believers as the guardians is, in the reading of the commentators, the taking that follows from the taking of God as the guardian, and the following is the act that the verse does in the form of the extension, since the extension of the guardianship from God to the messenger and to the believers is the extension that the relation of the one to God produces in the form of the natural consequence.

**The Occasion the Accounts Keep**

Some of the commentators take the verse as the act of the approving of the companion who was the native of the city, and the companion is the one who gave up the tie of the protection with the clans of the community and made the tie of the protection only to the messenger and the community of the messenger, and the approving is the act that the verse does to the declaration of the companion in the form of the confirming. The declaration of the companion is the declaration that the verse states in the form of the command, and the confirming is the act that the sūrah does in the verse after the declaration. The other of the accounts takes the verse as the answer to the complaint of the companions who felt the social cutting off that the two clans of the community put on them, and the complaint is the act that the verse answers in the form of the naming of the guardians, since the naming of the guardians is the act that takes the companions out of the state of the needing the protection of the clans, and the needing is the state that the complaint names.

**The Giving in the Bowing**

The description of the verse, which names the ones who keep up the prayer and give the alms in the state of the bowing, is the description that the commentators take in the two forms. In the first form, the description is the description of the believers in the whole, and the taking is the one that one of the commentators gives. In the second form, the description is the description of the believers who are the sincere in the state that is free of the hypocrisy, and the taking is the one that the two commentators give, and the mark of the sincerity is the mark that the verse of the believers as the guardians of one another carries in the form of the description. The doing of the two deeds in the state of the bowing may also point at the state of the lowliness in which the one does the religious deeds, and the pointing is the act that the reading does in the form of the state, and the verse of the command to keep up the prayer and pay the alms and bow with the ones who bow is the verse that joins the two deeds with the bowing in the same form. The reading that both of the traditions of the commentary report widely, and that stands behind the names of the two influential first commentators, takes the description as the reference of the verse to the one who was the son-in-law and the cousin of the messenger and the fourth of the caliphs, and the reading rests on the report of the incident in which the one gave his ring to the man who stood asking for the charity while the one was in the bowing of the prayer. In the form of this reading, the verse is the verse that puts the giving of the charity when it is asked in the form of the encouragement, and the verse is the verse that puts the following of the high mark of the virtue of the one in the form of the recommendation, and the mark is the mark of the refusing to put off the giving of the charity that is the needed, even to the end of the prayer. The minority of the reports takes the verse as the reference to the first of the caliphs, and the taking is the one that the report of the minority gives.

**The Title the Party Gives the Verse**

The one party of the community gives the verse the title of the verse of the guardianship, and the giving is the act that the party does in the form of the naming. The word of the guardian is the word that is usually taken in the sense of the protector or the friend, and the position of the guardian and the state that corresponds to it are also the concepts of the spirit that carry the particular weight for the party, and the weight is the one that the party keeps in the tradition of the terms. The party has carried the terms in the tradition of the usage to the reference of the single spiritual authority of the one who was the son-in-law and the right that follows from it to the taking over of the messenger as the spiritual and the political leader of the community, and the carrying is the act that the party does in the form of the claim. In the form of the claim, the verse is the verse that refers to the one as the guardian of the believers, after God and after the messenger, and the referring is the act that the party takes as one of the clear signs of the right authority of the one over the community, and the sign is the sign that the party keeps for the reading of the verse.

**The Reservation of the Other Party and the Weight of the Association**

A number of the writers of the other party, and these writers acknowledge the wide tie of this verse to the one who was the son-in-law, hold that the reports which defend the claim are not wholly sound in their chains, or that the reports do not carry out the interpretations of the right of authority that the party lays on them, and the holding is the act that the writers do in the form of the reservation. One of the later commentators denies that the word of the guardianship carries in this verse any meaning beyond the friendship and the mutual support, and turns away from the idea that the word refers to a kind of the spiritual authority, and the denying is the act that the commentator does on the ground of the report that the one did not put forward this verse in the favor of his own right to the caliphate in the assembly that was brought together to settle the matter of the caliphate after the passing of the second of the caliphs. The wide standing of the tie of this verse with the one, even in the commentaries of the other party and among some of the first of the authorities, is the thing that points strongly to the soundness of the tie in the early tradition of the commentary of the Book, and the pointing is the act that the standing does in the form of the evidence, since the evidence of the standing is the evidence that the early tradition of the commentary keeps for the reading of the verse.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:56
> Whoever allies themselves with Allah, His Messenger, and fellow believers, then it is certainly Allah’s party that will prevail.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Party of God Named in the Verse**

The verse describes the ones who follow the injunction of the two verses before, which is the injunction to avoid taking the protectors other than God, the messenger, and the believers, in the form of the name that the verse gives to them, and the name is the name of the party of God. The naming is the act that the verse does in the form of the answer to the prohibition, since the prohibition of the verse of the guardianship is the prohibition that leaves the one without the protectors, and the naming of the party is the act that the verse does to put the protectors in the one in the form of the party, and the party is the form that the protectors take when the one takes them in the order that the verse of the protectors states.

**The Word That History Has Taken**

The name of the party of God is the name that many of the groups in the history of the religion have adopted up to the present time, and the adopting is the act that the groups do in the form of the taking of the name of the verse. The adoption includes the movement and the political party of the contemporary land that carries the name in the anglicized rendering, and the carrying is the act that the movement does in the form of the title. The sūrah gives the name to the one who takes the guardianship in the form that the verse states, and the giving is the act that the sūrah does in the verse of the close of the account of the guardianship, since the account of the guardianship is the account that ends in the naming of the party that the sūrah keeps for the one who takes the protectors of the verse.

**The Prosperous Party in the Other Sūrah**

The name of the party of God is the name that the sūrah of the pen states in the verse where the party is described in the form of the three marks, and the three marks are the mark of the prosperity of the ones, the mark of the contentment of God with the ones, and the mark of the contentment of the ones with God. The verse of the sūrah of the pen puts the party of God in the contrast with the party of the one who leads astray, and the contrast is the contrast that the sūrah keeps in the two verses, since the verse before the verse of the party of God names the party of the one who leads astray in the form of the statement that the party of the one is the party that is bound to lose, and the verse of the party of God names the party of God in the form of the statement that the party of God is the party that is bound to succeed.

**The Victory That the Verse Promises**

The verse closes with the statement of the victory of the party of God, and the statement is the statement that the sūrah keeps for the close of the account of the guardianship in the form of the promise. The promise is the promise that the one who takes the protectors of the verse takes with the taking, and the taking is the act that the sūrah commands in the verse of the prohibition and the verse of the protectors. The account of the guardianship runs from the prohibition of the verse of the taking of the two communities to the naming of the party of God, and the account is the account that the sūrah keeps for the matter of the protectors in the form of the three verses, and the three verses together are the structure that the sūrah gives to the command of the taking of the protectors, which is the structure of the prohibition, the naming, and the promise of the victory.

**The Bond That the Party Keeps**

The party of God is the form that the bond of the loyalty takes when the bond is the bond of the one who takes the protectors of the verse, and the form is the form that the sūrah keeps for the state of the one who takes the guardianship. The bond of the loyalty is the bond that the Book names for the believers as the guardians of one another in the verse that describes the men and the women of the belief as the ones who encourage the good and forbid the evil, and the naming is the act that the Book does in the form of the description of the community of the belief. The verse of this account puts the bond in the form of the party, and the putting is the act that the sūrah does in the verse of the close, since the party is the form that the bond takes when the one who is of the bond takes the protectors of the verse in the order that the verse of the protectors states. The one who is of the party of God is the one who is not of the party that the verse of the prohibition names, and the two parties are the two forms that the sūrah keeps for the state of the one in the matter of the guardianship. The sūrah states the two parties in the two verses of the account, and the stating is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the contrast, since the contrast of the two parties is the contrast that the account of the guardianship keeps for the community that hears, and the community is to take the contrast as the contrast of its own state in the matter of the taking of the protectors.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:57
> O believers! Do not seek the guardianship of those given the Scripture before you and the disbelievers who have made your faith a mockery and amusement. And be mindful of Allah if you are ˹truly˺ believers.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Prohibition of the Guardians Who Mock**

The verse continues the discussion of the ones whom the believers should take as the protectors, and the continuation is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the second prohibition, since the first prohibition of the account of the guardianship is the prohibition of the taking of the two communities as the protectors, and this prohibition is the prohibition of the taking of the ones who take the religion in the mockery and the play. The ones who take the religion in the mockery and the play are, in the understanding of the account, the ones who feign the belief outwardly but harbor the disbelief inwardly, and the feigning is the act that the state of the one does in the form of the showing and the hiding. The enemy of the believers is not only the one who fights the religion, and the fighting is the act of the open hostility, but also the one who ridicules the religion, and the ridiculing is the act of the hidden hostility that the verse forbids in the form of the prohibition of the guardianship.

**The Mocking That the Book Names**

The Book mentions frequently the ones who mock the religion itself, and the mocking is the act that the verse of the mockers of the messengers names, since no messenger ever came to the people without being mocked. The Book mentions the mocking of the signs of God in the form of the statement that the end of the evildoers was the most evil for the denying and the mocking of the signs, and the Book mentions the mocking of the warnings of the destruction that God sends in the form of the statement that the ones who mocked were overtaken by what they used to mock. The Book warns that the mockers will eventually be destroyed by the thing that they used to mock, and the warning is the warning that the Book states in the several verses that name the overtaking of the mockers by the mockery. The taking of the religion as the play is the engaging in the religion vainly and without the commitment or the serious purpose, and the engaging is the act that the two verses of the taking of the faith as the mere play and the amusement name in the form of the charge.

**The Occasion the Accounts Give**

The account that is reported connects the passage to the hypocrites whom some of the Muslim believers had befriended, and the befriending is the act that the verse of the prohibition answers in the form of the warning to the Muslims to discontinue the close relations with the ones who mock the religion. The other of the accounts takes the passage as the reference to some of the members of the community of the book and the idolaters who would mock the Muslims when the Muslims made the prostration in the prayer, and the mocking of the prostration is the act that the account names as the form of the mockery, since the prostration is the act of the religion that the mocking takes in the form of the play.

**The Two Categories the Verse Names**

The verse mentions both the ones who were given the Book before the community of the sūrah, which are the ones of the community of the book who feign the belief in the religion of the sūrah, and the disbelievers, and the two are the two categories that the verse keeps in the form of the naming. The second of the categories is taken in the reading of one of the commentators as the separate category that refers to the idolaters, and the taking is the one that the word of the disbelievers gives in the context of the verse. For the foundational legal scholar of the school, this verse meant that the Muslims should not take the People of the Book as the allies, even against the idolaters, and the meaning is the one that the scholar takes in the form of the ruling, since the ruling of the scholar puts the two categories in the same prohibition without the distinction of the circumstance.

**The Circumstances of the New Community**

The command of the verse seems to pertain specifically to the circumstances of the new community of the religion in the city, and the circumstances are the circumstances of the community that is in the state of the struggle to establish itself. Some of the accounts say that the command was connected with the incident in which the messenger refused the help of some of the members of the community who offered to fight alongside him in the battle of the valley in the third year of the migration, and the refusing is the act that the account names as the form of the application of the command, since the refusing of the help of the ones who are in the state of the feigning is the act that the command of the verse requires. The similar incident is reported in the sūrah of the family in the account of the battle of the trench in the fifth year of the migration, and the reporting is the act that the Book does in the form of the parallel of the two incidents.

**The Two Prohibitions of the Account**

The sūrah keeps the two prohibitions of the account of the guardianship in the two verses, and the two are the prohibition of the taking of the two communities as the protectors and the prohibition of the taking of the ones who mock the religion as the protectors. The first of the two is the prohibition that the account states in the form of the naming of the two communities, and the second of the two is the prohibition that the account states in the form of the naming of the state of the mockery, and the two together are the structure that the sūrah gives to the matter of the guardianship. The structure is the structure of the prohibition that runs from the naming of the community to the naming of the state, and the running is the act that the sūrah does in the two verses, since the two together are the account that the sūrah keeps for the command of the not taking of the protectors in the form that the protectors are not the protectors of the verse.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:58
> When you call to prayer, they mock it in amusement. This is because they are a people without understanding. Who Deserves Resentment?

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Call That the Verse Grounds**

The verse is considered by the commentators as the scriptural basis of the ritual practice of the call to the prayer, and the practice is the practice that the technical term of the call names. This is the only verse of the Book that mentions the call to the prayer directly, and the directness is the act that the verse does in the form of the naming, although the verse does not use the technical term of the call in the naming, since the verse names the call in the form of the calling to the prayer and not in the form of the word that the later usage fixed for the call. The calling to the prayer is the act that the community does in the form of the public announcement, and the announcement is the act that the verse takes as the act that the ones who do not understand mock.

**The Call and the Institution of It**

The call to the prayer is made, in the ideal of the practice, publicly before each of the five daily prayers, and the making is the act that the practice keeps in the form of the announcement of the time. The text of the call in the usage of the majority is the text that opens with the proclamation of the greatness of God in the fourfold form, and takes up the two testimonies, which are the testimony of the oneness of God and the testimony of the messengership of the messenger, and closes with the summons to the prayer and to the prosperity, and the closing is the closing that the text of the call keeps in the form of the repetition of the proclamation. The usage of the other party adds to the text the line that testifies to the guardianship of the cousin after the testimony of the messengership, and adds the summons to the best of the acts after the summons to the prosperity. The practice was instituted, in the account of the commentators, only after the Muslims began the prayer in the direction of the house of the land, which was about two years after the migration from the city of the birth to the city of the refuge, and some of the companions are said to have had the dreams about such a call to the prayer, and several of the prominent companions indicated the need for the public announcement of the times of the prayer. Some of the accounts say that the messenger heard the call to the prayer during the night journey, and the hearing is the act that the account names in the form of the prefiguration of the institution.

**The Mocking of the Call**

The account that is reported in connection with the verse states that some of the members of the two communities in the city would mock the call to the prayer and cast the ill omens upon the messenger or the believers when they heard the call, and the mocking and the casting are the two acts that the account names as the form of the state of the ones who hear the call. The other of the accounts takes the mocking in the form that the disbelievers mocked the call to the prayer as the religious innovation, or as the thing that has the ugly sound, and the mocking in the two forms is the act that the verse answers in the form of the statement of the cause, since the cause of the mocking is the cause that the verse states at the close.

**The People Who Do Not Understand**

The verse closes with the statement of the cause of the mocking, and the cause is the cause of the people who do not understand. The statement is the statement that the sūrah keeps for the close of the account of the call in the form of the naming of the state, and the state is the state of the not understanding that the mocking does in the form of the act. One of the later commentators takes the statement in the sense that the ones of the real understanding know that the prayer is the best of all the acts of the human being, and the taking is the one that the commentator does on the ground of the saying that he quotes, which is the saying that the most noble of the movements is the prayer, and the most beneficial of the stillnesses is the fast, and the saying is the act that the commentator does in the form of the support of the statement of the verse.

**The Verse in the Account of the Guardians**

The verse stands in the sūrah after the prohibition of the guardians who mock, and the position is the position that gives the verse the function in the account of the guardianship. The prohibition of the verse before is the prohibition of the taking of the ones who mock the religion as the protectors, and this verse is the verse that tells the act of the mocking in the form of the mocking of the call to the prayer, and the two together are the account that the sūrah keeps for the state of the one who mocks the religion, since the mocking of the call is the mocking of the religion in the form that the religion does it in the act of the daily announcement, and the daily announcement is the act that the state of the one who mocks takes in the form of the habit of the mockery.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:59
> Say, ˹O Prophet,˺ “O People of the Book! Do you resent us only because we believe in Allah and what has been revealed to us and what was revealed before—while most of you are rebellious?”

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Question of the Vengeance**

The verse states the question in the form of the address to the People of the Book, and the question is the question of the reason of the vengeance that the community takes against the community of the sūrah. The question names the three beliefs that the community of the sūrah holds, and the three are the belief in God, the belief in the thing that was sent down to the community of the sūrah, and the belief in the thing that was sent down before the thing of the sūrah. The question is the question that puts the vengeance in the form of the thing that has no reason other than the belief, and the putting is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the charge, since the charge that the belief is the only reason of the vengeance is the charge that the question states against the community of the book.

**The Parallel of the Sorcerers**

The verse is similar in the form to the verse of the sūrah of the story of the one who claimed the lordship, where, after the sorcerers of the one repented and came to believe in the messenger who was sent to the people, the one threatened to kill them, and they answered with the statement that the rage of the one toward them was only because they believed in the signs of their Lord when the signs came to them. The parallel of the two answers is the parallel that the form of the question keeps, and the keeping is the act that the Book does in the form of the repetition of the form, since the form of the answer of the one who is threatened with the killing because of the belief is the form that the sūrah keeps in the verse of this account and in the verse of the story of the sorcerers.

**The Affirmation That Broke the Tie**

The report that is preserved considers the verse as the verse that came down in relation to some of the members of the community of the city who broke the tie with the messenger because of the affirmation of the messenger of the prophethood of the prophet who was sent to the people of the book, and the affirmation is the affirmation that the phrase of the thing that was sent down before references, since the thing that was sent down before is the book that carries the account of the prophet. The breaking of the tie is the act that the members of the community did in the form of the refusal, and the refusal is the refusal of the affirmation of the prophethood that the members of the community did not accept, and the not accepting is the act that the report names as the cause of the breaking.

**The Iniquity That Is the Root**

The verse indicates that the rejection of the messenger by the community had its root in the iniquity of many among them, and the iniquity is the iniquity that is the own deviation of the many from the religion and the laws of God. The deviation is the act that the many did in the form of the departing from the book of the community, and the departing is the act that compelled the many to harbor the ill will toward the messenger, the message of the Book, and the community of the sūrah. The harboring of the ill will is the act that the iniquity produces in the form of the feeling, and the feeling is the act that the verse names in the closing phrase in the form of the statement that the many are the iniquitous, since the statement is the act that the verse does to put the root of the rejection in the state of the one who is rejected and not in the message that is rejected.

**The Question for the Community That Hears**

The sūrah puts the question in the account so that the community that hears takes the question as the question of the state of the one who is rejected for the belief, and the taking is the act that the sūrah expects of the reader. The question is the question that the community of the sūrah is to hear as the question of the relation of the belief and the rejection, and the relation is the relation that the sūrah states in the form of the charge, since the charge that the vengeance has no reason other than the belief is the charge that the community is to take as the charge of the state of the one who takes vengeance for the belief of the other. The verse that follows is the verse that answers the question in the form of the statement of the worse recompense, and the answering is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the continuation of the charge.

**The Three Beliefs in the Form of the Answer**

The question of the verse names the three beliefs in the form of the answer to the vengeance, and the three together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the state of the belief of the community. The belief in God is the first of the three, and the belief in the thing that was sent down to the community of the sūrah is the second of the three, and the belief in the thing that was sent down before is the third of the three, and the order of the three is the order that the sūrah keeps in the form of the address. The question is the question that puts the three beliefs in the form of the only reason of the vengeance, and the putting is the act that the sūrah does against the community of the book in the form of the charge, since the charge that the belief is the reason of the vengeance is the charge that the question states in the form that the community is to hear.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:60
> Say, ˹O Prophet,˺ “Shall I inform you of those who deserve a worse punishment from Allah ˹than the rebellious˺? It is those who earned Allah’s condemnation and displeasure—some being reduced to apes and pigs and worshippers of false gods. These are far worse in rank and farther astray from the Right Way.”

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Irony of the Recompense**

The verse states the question in the form of the offer of the information, and the information is the information of the thing that is worse than the thing of the verse before. The word that the verse uses for the recompense is the word that usually denotes the positive recompense, which is the reward rather than the punishment, and the use of the word to introduce the punishment of God is the use that is the ironic, since the irony of the word is the act that the verse does in the form of the charge. The tone of the verse is the tone that the Book keeps in the verses where the messenger is told to give the glad tidings of the punishment to come, and the tone is the tone that the verse of the glad tidings of the painful punishment to the hypocrites keeps in the same form, since the giving of the glad tidings of the punishment is the act that the Book does in the form of the irony, and the irony is the act that the verse of this account does in the form of the word of the recompense.

**The Cursed and the Wrath**

The verse names the ones whom God has cursed, and the cursing is the act that the commentators take in the sense of the exiling of the ones from the mercy of God, and the exiling is the act that the word of the curse carries in the form of the meaning. The wrath of God is the wrath that the verse names in the form of the thing that befalls the ones, and the befalling is the act that the verse states for the ones in the form of the state, since the state of the one on whom the wrath of God is the state that the verse puts in the form of the naming of the recompense. The two marks together are the form that the verse keeps for the state of the ones who are the worse in the recompense of God.

**The Apes and the Swine**

The phrase of the verse that names the ones whom God made some of them into the apes is likely the reference to the saying of God to the members of the community who violated the sabbath, and the saying is the saying that the two verses of the story of the sabbath name in the form of the command to be the apes that are the disgraced. The making of the ones into the apes and the swine can be understood, in the reading of the commentators, as the metaphor for the state of the ones who deliberately violate the religious law, and the state is the state in either of the two worlds, and the metaphor is the act that the reading does in the form of the meaning. The making can also be seen as the reference to the changing of the hearts of the ones and the hardening of them, and the hardening is the act that the several verses of the Book name in the form of the state of the hearts, and the state leads to the proneness to the heedlessness of the ones. Some of the accounts say that the apes refer to the ones who broke the sabbath, and the swine refer to the ones among the followers of the prophet who betrayed him during his last meal, and the referring is the act that the account does in the form of the specification of the two.

**The False Deities They Worship**

The verse names the worship of the false deities in the form of the charge, and the charge is the charge that the verse of the sūrah of the family states in the same form, since the verse of the sūrah of the family names the ones who were given the portion of the Book and who believe in the idols and the false deities. The reference of the charge may be the reference to the worship of the community of the people of the book of the calf of the gold, and the reference is the one that one of the commentators gives in the form of the incident, since the incident of the calf is the incident that the two accounts of the Book tell in the form of the making of the calf and the worship of it.

**The Worsening of the State**

The verse closes with the statement of the state of the ones in the form of the comparison, and the comparison is the comparison of the state of the ones with the state of the thing of the verse before. The ones are the ones who are in the worse situation, and the worse is the act that the verse states in the form of the naming of the state, and the ones are the ones who are further astray from the right way, and the being further astray is the act that the verse states in the form of the adding to the naming. The two statements together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the close of the charge, since the charge that the verse before stated in the form of the question is the charge that this verse answers in the form of the statement of the worse recompense, and the answering is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the completion of the account of the vengeance and the iniquity.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:61
> When they come to you ˹believers˺ they say, “We believe.” But they are committed to disbelief when they enter and when they leave. And Allah knows what they hide.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Coming in the Claim of the Belief**

The verse states the coming of the group to the messenger in the form of the claim, and the claim is the claim of the belief that the group makes in the act of the coming. The account that is reported connects the verse to the group of the members of the community of the city who came to the messenger claiming the belief in him and in the message that he brought, and the claiming is the act that the group did in the form of the showing of the belief. The claiming is not the claiming that the state of the group carried, and the not carrying is the act that the sūrah states in the form of the charge, since the charge that the claim of the belief is the claim that does not stand on the state of the belief is the charge that the verse keeps for the act of the coming.

**The Entering and the Leaving in the State**

The verse states the two acts of the group in the form of the entering and the leaving, and the two acts are the acts that the group does in the state that is the state of the disbelief. The group certainly entered with the disbelief, and the entering is the act of the coming to the messenger in the state of the disbelief, and the group certainly left with the disbelief, and the leaving is the act of the going from the messenger in the state of the disbelief. The two acts together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the state of the group, since the state is the state that the act of the coming did not take in the form of the state of the belief, and the not taking is the act that the verse states in the form of the naming of the two acts, which is the naming of the entering and the leaving in the one state.

**The Concealing That the Knowledge of God Reaches**

The verse closes with the statement of the knowledge of God for the thing that the group concealed, and the concealing is the act of the heart that the act of the mouth did not do in the form of the showing. The group concealed the state of the disbelief in the act of the coming, and the concealing is the act that the sūrah names in the form of the thing that was the thing of the state, since the state of the group in the act of the coming was the state of the disbelief under the claim of the belief. The knowledge of God for the concealing is the knowledge that the verse states in the form of the certainty, and the certainty is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the statement, since the statement that God best knows the thing that the group concealed is the statement that the sūrah keeps for the state of the one who comes in the claim that is not the claim of the state.

**The Making Known of the State**

The account that the commentators preserve states that God made the state of the hypocrisy of the group known to the messenger, and the making known is the act that God did in the form of the revealing of the state to the messenger. The revealing is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the knowledge, and the knowledge is the knowledge that the messenger takes of the state of the one who comes to him in the claim of the belief. The verse of this account is the verse that states the making known in the form of the statement of the knowledge of God, and the stating is the act that the sūrah does so that the community that hears takes the statement as the statement of the state of the one who comes in the claim, since the claim of the one who comes is the claim that the community is to take in the form that the verse of the making known keeps for it.

**The Verse in the Account of the Coming**

The verse stands in the sūrah in the middle of the account of the coming of the group to the messenger, and the account is the account that the sūrah keeps from the verse of the question of the vengeance to the verse of the charge of the shackled hand. The verse of the question before is the verse that states the charge of the vengeance in the form of the address to the People of the Book, and the verse of the charge after is the verse that states the claim of the group in the form of the charge, and this verse is the verse that tells the act of the coming in the form of the claim and the state, and the three together are the structure that the sūrah gives to the account of the relation of the messenger and the community of the book, which is the structure of the charge of the vengeance, the telling of the coming, and the charge of the state. The sūrah keeps the account of the coming in the form of the three verses, and the keeping is the act that the sūrah does in the structure of the charge, the telling, and the charge, since the three together are the account that the community that hears is to take as the account of the relation of the messenger and the community of the book in the matter of the coming of the group to the messenger in the claim of the belief that is not the belief.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:62
> You see many of them racing towards sin, transgression, and consumption of forbidden gain. Evil indeed are their actions!

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Hastening That the Vision Takes**

The verse opens with the statement of the vision of the messenger, and the vision is the vision of the many of the group in the act of the hastening. The hastening is the act that the many do in the form of the running to the two things, and the two things are the sin and the enmity. The sin is the sin that the commentators take in the several glosses, and the glosses are the gloss of the lying, and the gloss of the disbelief, and the taking of the sin in the sense that it may relate to every kind of the sin, and the three together are the forms that the word of the sin keeps in the readings of the verse. The enmity is the enmity that the commentators take in the several referents, and the referents are the hostility toward the messenger, the general wrongdoing of the many, and the passing of the limits that God set, and the three together are the forms that the word of the enmity keeps in the readings of the verse.

**The Consuming of the Forbidden**

The verse states the third act of the many in the form of the consuming, and the consuming is the consuming of the thing that is the forbidden. The act of the consuming is the act that the sūrah has already named in the verse of the taking of the unlawful, and the naming is the act that the verse of the account of the judging states in the form of the charge of the bribery and the illicit gain, since the taking of the unlawful in that verse is the taking that is widely reported as the reference to the gain that is not the gain of the law and the taking that is the taking of the bribe. The verse of this account puts the consuming in the form of the third act of the many, and the putting is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the adding to the charge, since the charge of the many runs from the hastening to the sin to the hastening to the enmity to the consuming of the forbidden, and the three acts together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the state of the many.

**The Enmity in the Several Forms**

The enmity of the verse is the enmity that takes the several forms in the account of the sūrah and in the other sūrahs, and the several forms are the forms that the enmity of the group takes toward the community of the sūrah. The first of the forms is the form of the hostility toward the messenger in the person of the messenger, and the hostility is the act that the account of the sūrah tells in the form of the several incidents of the relation of the group and the messenger. The second of the forms is the form of the general wrongdoing of the many in the act that the many do against the law of the book of the group. The third of the forms is the form of the passing of the limits that God set in the act of the group that goes beyond the measure that the law gives, and the three forms together are the state that the word of the enmity carries in the verse, since the state of the enmity of the group is the state that the several forms take in the one word.

**The Closing That Names the Doing**

The verse closes with the statement of the deed of the many in the form of the charge, and the charge is the charge of the evil of the doing. The doing is the act that the closing names in the form of the word of the doing, and the naming is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the statement of the state of the many. The statement of the evil of the doing is the statement that the verse keeps for the close of the account of the many, and the keeping is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the parallel to the closing of the verse after, since the verse after closes with the word of the working in the form of the charge of the sages, and the two words together are the two forms that the sūrah keeps for the two states of the group.

**The Two Verses in the Contrast**

The sūrah keeps this verse and the verse after in the form of the contrast, and the contrast is the contrast of the many and the sages. This verse names the many of the group in the state of the hastening and the consuming, and the verse after names the sages and the scholars of the group in the state of the not forbidding, and the two together are the account that the sūrah keeps for the state of the group in the form of the two verses. The contrast is the contrast that the sūrah does in the form of the word of the doing and the word of the working, and the two words are the two forms that the sūrah gives to the two states of the group, since the state of the many is the state of the doing of the sin, and the state of the sages is the state of the working of the not forbidding in the act that the knowledge of the sages requires.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:63
> Why do their rabbis and scholars not forbid them from saying what is sinful and consuming what is unlawful? Evil indeed is their inaction!

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Question of the Forbidding**

The verse opens with the question that is the form of the charge against the leaders of the group, and the charge is the charge of the not forbidding. The question names the two matters that the leaders are to forbid, and the two matters are the sinful speech and the consuming of the forbidden. The word of the sages is the word that the sūrah used in the verse of the judges of the Torah, and the word of the scholars is the word that the sūrah used in the same verse for the religious scholars of the group, and the two words together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the leaders of the group in the account of the charge. The question is the question that puts the not forbidding in the form of the thing that the leaders do, and the putting is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the charge, since the charge that the leaders do not forbid the two matters is the charge that the question states against the one who is the leader in the state of the knowing.

**The Sin of the Omission**

The verse states the sin of the leaders in the form of the omission, and the omission is the act of the not doing of the forbidding in the state of the knowing of the leaders. The leaders are the ones who know the two matters in the state of the knowledge that the position of the leaders gives, and the not doing of the forbidding is the act that the knowledge of the leaders does not excuse, since the act of the forbidding is the act that the knowledge of the leaders requires in the form of the duty. The sin of the omission on the part of the sages who know is the sin that can be considered the more serious and the more deeply rooted than the sin of the common people who do not have the wide religious knowledge, and the considering is the act that the account does in the form of the weighing of the two sins, since the weighing of the sin of the knowing one and the sin of the not knowing one is the weighing that the account of the two verses does in the form of the contrast.

**The Doing and the Working**

The sūrah keeps the two verses of the account in the form of the parallel of the closings, and the parallel is the parallel of the word of the doing in the verse of the many and the word of the working in the verse of the sages. The verse of the many states the evil of the doing in the form of the charge of the general sinfulness and the lack of the belief of the common sinner, and the doing is the act that the common sinner does in the form of the sin. The verse of the sages states the evil of the working in the form of the charge of the thing that the sages bring about, and the working is the act that the refusal of the sages to forbid the wrongful behavior brings about in the state where the sages were able to change the behavior by the act of ruling correctly on the basis of the knowledge. The difference of the two words is the difference that the sūrah does in the form of the parallel, and the parallel is the act that the account of the two verses keeps for the state of the two parts of the group.

**The Verse the Authorities Named**

Some of the early authorities, and among them the early commentator and the commentator who passed in the hundred and fifth year of the migration, are reported to have considered this verse to be among the most severe and the most frightening of the verses of the Book, and the considering is the act that the authorities did in the form of the naming of the verse. The naming is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the weight, and the weight is the weight that the verse carries in the state of the warning that the verse implies for the one who has the religious knowledge and does not live up to the moral responsibility that the knowledge puts on the one. The warning is the warning that the verse keeps for the sages of the community that hears, and the keeping is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the address of the state, since the state of the one who has the knowledge and does not do the forbidding is the state that the verse of the charge names in the form of the working of the evil.

**The Two Matters of the Charge**

The sūrah keeps the two matters of the charge in the verse in the form of the naming, and the two matters are the matter of the speech and the matter of the consuming. The speech is the speech that is the sinful in the act of the group that names the wrongdoing in the word, and the consuming is the consuming of the forbidden in the act of the group that takes the thing that is not the thing of the law. The two matters together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the charge of the sages, since the charge that the sages do not forbid is the charge that the sūrah states in the form of the two matters, and the two matters are the matters that the account of the verse before named in the form of the hastening and the consuming of the many, and the naming of the two matters in the two verses is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the parallel of the two states of the group.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:64
> ˹Some among˺ the Jews said, “Allah is tight-fisted.” May their fists be tied and they be condemned for what they said. Rather, He is openhanded, giving freely as He pleases. That which has been revealed to you ˹O Prophet˺ from your Lord will only cause many of them to increase in wickedness and disbelief. We have stirred among them hostility and hatred until the Day of Judgment. Whenever they kindle the fire of war, Allah puts it out. And they strive to spread corruption in the land. And Allah does not like corruptors.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Charge of the Fettered Hand**

The verse states the claim of the group in the form of the address to the hand of God, and the claim is the claim of the fettering of the hand. The image of the fettered hand is the image that the Book uses in the account of the torments of the next world in the form of the helplessness of the ones who are faced with the punishment of God, and the image is also the image that the Book associates with the stinginess in the verse of the command against the two states of the hand, and the stinginess is the meaning that is the intended meaning in this verse, since the meaning of the stinginess is the meaning that the claim of the group takes in the form of the suggestion that God holds back the provision and the bounty, at least from the group.

**The Response That Turns the Charge**

The verse states the response of God to the claim in the form of the turning of the charge back on the ones who make the charge, and the turning is the act that the response does in the form of the statement that the hands of the ones are the fettered. The turning of the charge is the act that the Book does in the form of the reflexive nature of the sin and the falsehood, and the reflexive nature is the nature that the verse of the deceiving states in the form of the statement that the ones who seek to deceive God and the believers deceive none but themselves, since the wrong that the one intends for the other is the wrong that comes back on the one in the form of the thing that the one does not see. The statement of the fettering can also be read in the form of the wish, which is the wish that the hands of the ones be the fettered from all of the good things, and the reading is the one that one of the commentators gives. The verse states the cursing of the ones for the thing that they say, and the cursing is the cursing that is widely understood in the sense of the exiling of the ones from the mercy of God, and the exiling is the act that the word of the curse carries in the form of the meaning.

**The Saying the Accounts Keep**

One of the later commentators holds that the group, in the form of the doctrine of the group, does not make the claim of the fettering of the hand of God, and the holding is the act that the commentator does on the ground of the knowledge of the group of the might and the power of God and of the impossibility of the truth of the claim. The commentator suggests that the ones of the group who said the claim did not say it in the earnest, but said it in the way of the mocking of the community of the sūrah, and the mocking is the act that the commentator names in the form of the several accounts. In one of the accounts, the ones take a question of the Book, which is the question of the lending of the good loan to God, and the question is the question that the Book states in the several verses, and the ones pretend the understanding of the question in the literal form, which is the form of the need of God for the loan of the servants, and argue in the facetious form that the hand of God is the fettered by the need, in order to put the question of the Book in the form of the absurd. In the other of the accounts, the ones see the dire situation of the money in which the early community of the city found itself, and say in the mocking form that the hand of the God of the messenger is the fettered, since the help of God for the community in the material form did not appear to the ones. Some of the commentators suggest that when the fortunes of the group of the city changed for the worse with the rise of the religion of the sūrah in the city and in the land, the ones said the claim in the meaning that God now holds back the blessings from the group as the community, and the saying is the act that the commentators take in the form of the reference. The claim of the ones may also refer or relate to the claim of the ones elsewhere, which is the claim that the Fire will not touch them except for the number of the days, and the claim is the claim that the two verses of the sūrah of the family and the sūrah of the table spread name in the form of the statement, since the claim suggests a certain limitation on the part of God, and the limitation is the act that the claim of the fettering takes in the form of the meaning.

**The Two Hands That Are Wide Open**

The verse contrasts the claim of the ones with the image of the two hands of God that are the wide open, and the image is the image of the hand that is the open and the giving in the act that God does as He wills. The description of the hands of God as the wide open is the description that forms part of the basis of the Book for the Name of God that is the name of the Open-Handed, and the naming is the act that the Book does in the form of the title. The several verses of the Book state that God spreads out the provision and straitens it for whom He wills, and the stating is the act that the Book does in the form of the repetition of the description, since the description of the spreading and the straitening is the description that the Book keeps for the act of the giving of the provision. The image of the two hands of God in the form that appears to be the image of the likeness occurs in the verse where God states that He created the first of the people of the book with His two hands, and the occurring is the act that the Book does in the form of the repetition of the image. Some of the interpreters consider all the references of the Book to the hand of God, in the singular or in the dual, as the metaphors for the blessings that God gives to His creatures, or for the strength and the power of God, and the considering is the act that the interpreters do in the form of the meaning, and the verse that describes the three of the prophets as the men of the strength is the verse that the interpreters take in the form of the reference, since the word of the strength is the word that is the word of the possession of the hands in the literal form. Some of the scholars of the religion argued that the references of the Book to the hand or the hands of God have to be understood in the literal form, even if the hand of God cannot be considered of the same nature as the hand of the human being, and the arguing is the act that the scholars do in the form of the rejection of the interpretation of the likeness. Some of the commentators suggest that the claim of the ones, which uses the singular of the hand, is refuted by the statement of the verse, which uses the dual of the two hands, in order to indicate the great abundance of the provision of God, and the suggesting is the act that the commentators do in the form of the reading, although one of the early commentators rejects the reading, and the rejecting is the act that the commentator does on the ground of the rule that the dual form always represents the two specific things and cannot be made to signify the simple abundance. If the hand of God can be seen as the metaphor and the symbol for the blessings that God gives, then the two hands of God may be the metaphor for the two different kinds of the blessings that God may give, and the kinds are the blessings of this world and the blessings of the next, or the outward blessings and the inward blessings, or the blessings of the granting of the benefit and the blessings of the preventing of the harm.

**The Revelation That Increases the Disbelief**

The verse states the idea that is the paradoxical in the form of the statement, and the statement is the statement that the revelation of God, although the revelation is the thing that is meant to bring the guidance, may lead some of the people farther into the astray. The idea is the idea that the Book states in the several verses of the other sūrahs, and the stating is the act that the Book does in the form of the repetition of the statement, since the statement of the leading astray of the revelation is the statement that the Book keeps for the act of the message on the one who does not take the message in the form of the guidance. The revelation that the messenger receives from God serves, in the statement of the verse, to increase many of the group in the defiance and the disbelief, and the increasing is the act that the revelation does in the form of the charge. The revelation and the prophecy are referred to in the several places of the Book as the Criterion, and the criterion is the criterion that part of the function of the revelation is to distinguish and separate the believers from the disbelievers, and the distinguishing is the act that the Book does in the form of the naming of the function. The disbelievers are frequently described in the Book as the ones who are repelled by the message of God, and the description is the description that the verse of the frightened asses that flee from the lion keeps in the form of the image, since the image of the fleeing is the image that the Book gives to the state of the one who turns away from the reminder.

**The Enmity, the Flame, and the Corruption**

The verse states the punishment of God for the defiance and the disbelief of the group in the form of the sowing of the enmity and the hatred among the group until the day of the resurrection, and the sowing is the act that God does in the form of the casting between the members of the group. The verse of this sūrah that states the sowing of the hostility and the enmity among the ones of the other community is the verse that the sūrah keeps in the same form, since the sowing is the act that the sūrah does for the two communities in the form of the parallel. Some of the commentators suggest that the sowing is the sowing between the two communities of the book, and the suggesting is the act that the commentator does in the form of the reading. The group repeatedly kindles the flame of the war, and God extinguishes the flame, and the kindling and the extinguishing are the two acts that the verse keeps in the form of the parallel of the act of the group and the act of God. Some of the commentators interpret the flame as the reference to the unsuccessful hostility of the group toward the mission of the messenger, and the other of the commentators sees the flame as the reference to the series of the military losses of the group from the time of the invasions of the two empires that are the empires of the assyrians and the babylonians, and the invasions are the invasions that the Book alludes to in the verse of the two warnings. The verse closes with the statement of the working of the corruption upon the earth, and the working is the act that the verse forbids in the form of the command, and the verse of the spreading of the corruption on the land and the sea as the result of the deed of the hands of the people is the verse that the Book keeps for the matter of the corruption in the same form, and the closing statement that God does not love the workers of the corruption is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the naming of the state of God for the state of the one who works the corruption.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:65
> Had the People of the Book only been faithful and mindful ˹of Allah˺, We would have certainly absolved them of their sins and admitted them into the Gardens of Bliss.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Counterfactual of the Belief**

The verse opens with the statement of the condition that was not the condition of the fact, and the condition is the condition of the belief and the righteousness of the People of the Book. The statement of the condition in the form of the thing that did not happen is the form that the sūrah keeps for the verse, and the keeping is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the counterfactual, since the counterfactual is the form that the sūrah gives to the promise that the condition that was not fulfilled carries. The condition names the two states of the People of the Book, and the two states are the state of the belief and the state of the righteousness, and the two together are the form that the verse keeps for the state of the community that the verse addresses.

**The Belief That the Verse Names**

The belief that the verse names is, in the reading of the most of the commentators, the belief in the Book of the sūrah and the prophethood of the messenger, and the reading is the one that the form of the address gives, since the address is the address to the People of the Book in the presence of the messenger and the Book. The word of the belief is sometimes used in the Book to refer to the general act of the believing in God and the last day among the members of the two communities as well as the members of the community of the sūrah, and the use is the use that the two verses of the verses of the believers, the Jews, the Christians, and the Sabians carry in the form of the statement that whoever truly believes in God and the last day and does the good has the reward with the Lord. The term of the believer is most commonly used in the Book to refer to the one who believes in all of the prophets and the books, necessarily including the messenger of the sūrah and the Book of the sūrah, and the referring is the act that the verse of the command of the faith in God, the messenger, the book, and the scriptures that came before keeps in the form of the requirement, since the requirement of the faith in all of the prophets and the books is the requirement that puts the believer in the form of the member of the community of the sūrah.

**The Absolving and the Gardens**

The verse states the two acts of God that the condition of the belief and the righteousness carries, and the two acts are the act of the absolving of the evil deeds and the act of the admitting to the Gardens of the delight. The absolving is the act that God does to the evil deeds of the community in the form of the removal, and the admitting is the act that God does to the community in the form of the putting into the Gardens of the delight, and the two together are the form that the verse keeps for the promise of the state of the community in the next world. The counterfactual of the verse keeps the promise in the form of the condition that was not the condition of the fact, and the keeping is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the statement, since the statement of the promise in the form of the thing that was not done is the statement that the sūrah uses for the community that did not take the condition in the form of the act.

**The Parallel Verses of the Message**

The Book states the similar message concerning the People of the Book or the earlier peoples in the several verses, and the similar message is the message of the counterfactual of the belief and the righteousness. The verse of the sūrah of the family states the message in the form of the statement that if the people of the book had been faithful and mindful, there would have been the better reward from God, and the stating is the act that the sūrah of the family does in the form of the counterfactual. The verse of the sūrah of the story of the one who claimed the lordship states the message in the form of the statement that had the people of the societies been faithful and mindful, God would have overwhelmed them with the blessings from the heaven and the earth, and the stating is the act that the sūrah of the story does in the form of the counterfactual, and the two verses together are the form that the Book keeps for the message of the belief and the righteousness in the form of the thing that was not the act of the peoples.

**The Verse in the Account of the Sūrah**

The verse stands in the sūrah between the verse of the charge of the group and the verse of the observing of the two books, and the position is the position that gives the verse the function in the account of the community of the book. The verse of the charge before is the verse that states the claim of the group in the form of the charge, and the verse of the observing after is the verse that states the condition of the observing of the books in the form of the counterfactual of the nourishment, and this verse is the verse that states the condition of the belief and the righteousness in the form of the counterfactual of the absolving and the gardens, and the three together are the structure that the sūrah gives to the account of the community of the book, which is the structure of the charge of the state, the counterfactual of the belief, and the counterfactual of the observing.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:66
> And had they observed the Torah, the Gospel, and what has been revealed to them from their Lord, they would have been overwhelmed with provisions from above and below. Some among them are upright, yet many do nothing but evil.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Observing of the Three Things**

The verse opens with the statement of the condition of the observing of the three things, and the three things are the Torah, the Gospel, and the thing that was sent down to the community from the Lord. The sūrah indicates the importance of the observing of the two books by the two communities in the several verses, and the verses are the verses of the command of the judging by the book and the verse of the address to the community at the close of the account of the sūrah, and the indicating is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the repetition of the command. The verse of this account puts the observing of the two books in the form of the condition of the nourishment, and the putting is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the counterfactual, since the counterfactual of the nourishment is the form that the sūrah keeps for the promise of the state of the community that observes the books.

**The Two Readings of the Observing**

The observing of the two books is understood by some of the commentators in the limited sense, and the limited sense is the sense of the upholding of the covenant of God that was established in the two books in the general way, and the upholding is the act that does not preclude the following of the messenger by the two communities. In the form of the limited sense, the upholding of the covenant would require the following of the messenger of the sūrah, and the requiring is the act that the Book does in the form of the statement, since the Book indicates that the messenger was inscribed in the Torah and the Gospel in the verse where the description of the messenger is the description that the two communities find in the two books, and in the verse where the prophet of the Gospel gives the good news of the messenger who comes after him with the name that the verse names. The other of the readings takes the observing in the sense of the continuing to uphold the rituals and the laws that are prescribed in the two books, and the upholding is the act that the reading keeps in the form of the statement of the verse of the law and the way, since the verse of the law and the way states that God appointed for each of the communities the law and the way, and the appointing is the act that the verse does in the form of the command of the observing of the way of the community.

**The Thing That Was Sent Down to Them**

The verse requires the observing of the thing that was sent down to the community from the Lord, and the thing is the thing that the commentators take in the two readings. In the reading of many of the early commentators, the thing is the Book of the sūrah, and the reading is the one that the two commentators give. In the other of the readings, the thing may be the reference to certain of the books of the prophets that are found in the scriptures of the people of the book and are outside of the Torah in the narrow sense, and the reference is the one that the commentators give in the form of the book that is the book of the prophet who was sent to the people of the book after the book of the law. For the two communities to observe both of their own books and the Book of the sūrah means, in the reading of some of the commentators, the following of the teachings in the two books that were either the teachings that the Book of the sūrah endorsed or the teachings that the Book of the sūrah did not declare the false, and the following is the act that the reading keeps in the form of the requirement.

**The Nourishment from Above and Beneath**

The verse states the promise of the nourishment in the form of the counterfactual, and the nourishment is the nourishment that the community would have received from above them and from beneath their feet. The most of the commentators read the statement of the nourishment as the image of the prosperity of the world, and the image is the image of the rain that God would have sent from the heavens and the vegetation of the nourishment that God would have brought forth from the earth, and the image is the act that the commentators take in the form of the worldly meaning. The image of the nourishment can be read as the counterpart of the prosperity of the spirit that the verse of the belief and the righteousness before states for the community in the form of the gardens, and the counterpart is the act that the reading does in the form of the parallel. The image can also be read as the response to the claim of the verse before, which is the claim of the fettering of the hand of God, since the claim of the fettering suggests the holding back of the worldly provision by God, and the response is the act that the verse does in the form of the statement of the nourishment. The metaphor of the prosperity can be read in the form of the allegory of the spirit in relation to the statement of the verse that the two communities follow their own books and the thing that was sent down to them, and the allegory is the act that the reading does in the form of the meaning. In the form of the allegory, the nourishment from beneath the feet of the community is the teaching of their own books, which is the seed of the spirit that the community has already sown in the hearts, and the nourishment from above them is the Book of the sūrah, which is the rain of the life that gives, and the two together are the things that were sent down to the community from the Lord. The rain gives the direct nourishment to the human being alongside the crops that come from the ground, and the rain nourishes the crops themselves, and the allegory of the rain keeps the same form for the message of the Book of the sūrah, which provides the direct nourishment of the spirit to the community and the indirect nourishment of the spirit by the bringing of the new life to their own books.

**The Moderate Community**

The verse states the fact that there remains among the two communities the moderate community, and the community is the community that takes the middle course between the extremes in the act of the religion. The word of the community here is the word that is different from the word that the Book uses to describe the community of the sūrah as the upright community in the verse of the qiblah, and the difference is the act that the verse does in the form of the naming. The descriptions of the moderation and the balance are associated in the several ways with the thing that is the good and the proper in the Book, and the associating is the act that the Book does in the form of the several verses that name the middle course. In the reading that is consistent with the interpretation of the following of the books and the acceptance of the message of the Book of the sūrah, some of the commentators understand the moderate as the description of the members of the two communities who moderate their belief in a way that is consistent with the thing that was sent down to them, and the moderating is the act that the commentators take in the form of the reading of the state of the belief. In the form of the reading, the moderate community is the community that neither exaggerates the station of the prophet of the Gospel in the form of the considering of him as the son of God, nor underestimates the spiritual rank of the prophet in the form of the refusing to consider him the prophet of God, and sees the prophet as the prophet that God sent in the form that the Book of the sūrah presents him.
