The statement of the verse may also be read in the conjunction with the passages of the Book that describe the members of the two communities who are the just in the dealings with the community of the sūrah, and the dealing of the entrusting of the great amount of the gold and the returning of it is the dealing that the verse of the just keeps in the form of the description, and the passage of the middle course between the wrongdoing and the being foremost in the good deeds is the passage that the Book keeps for the state of the moderate in the same form. Some of the commentators suggest that the moderate community may refer to the members of the two communities who became the believers by the embracing of the religion of the sūrah, and the suggesting is the act that the commentators do in the form of the reading, although the reading does not seem to fit with the description of the taking of the middle course, and the not fitting is the act that the account states in the form of the objection.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:67
> O Messenger! Convey everything revealed to you from your Lord. If you do not, then you have not delivered His message. Allah will ˹certainly˺ protect you from the people. Indeed, Allah does not guide the people who disbelieve.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Command of the Conveying in the Middle of the Account**

The verse stands in the middle of the long discussion of the community of the book that the sūrah carries, and the position is the position that gives the verse the function in the account of the community. Some of the commentators suggest that the message which the messenger may have been the hesitant to convey is the message of the criticism of the other religious communities, and the suggesting is the act that the commentator does in the form of the reading, since the reading that the criticism of the communities is the message of the verse is the reading that the position of the verse in the middle of the discussion of the communities gives. The command of the conveying is the command that the sūrah states in the form of the address to the messenger, and the stating is the act that the sūrah does in the middle of the account, since the account of the community of the book is the account that the command of the conveying stands in.

**The Threat of the Not Conveying**

The verse states the threat in the form of the condition of the not conveying, and the threat is the threat of the not having conveyed the message of God. One of the commentators holds that the condition carries the meaning that the hiding of the smallest portion of the revealed thing by the messenger would stand in the place of the hiding of the whole, and the holding is the act that the commentator does in the form of the reading of the condition, since the reading that the part takes the place of the whole is the reading that the form of the threat gives. One of the reports keeps the statement that the messenger told the community of the threat that God carried against the one who withholds the revealed thing, and the threat is the threat of the punishment of God on the one who does not do the conveying of all that came to him, and the keeping is the act that the report does in the form of the statement of the threat. The wife of the messenger is reported to have put this verse in the form of the answer to the claim of the concealing, and the claim is the claim of the ones who say that the messenger held back a portion of the thing that came to him, and the putting is the act that the report of the wife does in the form of the refutation, since the verse that commands the conveying of all is the verse that the claim of the holding back does not stand against in the form that the verse keeps.

**The Fear of the People and the Protection of God**

The assurance of the verse that God will protect the messenger from the people is the assurance that carries the implication that the messenger was in the state of the fear of the reaction of the people to the message in some of the times, and the implication is the act that the assurance does in the form of the suggestion of the state. One of the reports keeps the statement that in the time when the messenger was in the city of the birth and the followers were in the state of the constant danger from the tribe of the city, the messenger held back a portion of the thing that came to him from the state of the danger, and the holding back is the act that the report names as the form of the state of the messenger before the strengthening of the community. Once the community of the messenger took the state of the strength, the command of this verse put the conveying of all of the revealed thing in the form of the duty of the messenger, and the putting is the act that the verse does in the form of the command that follows the strengthening. The other of the reports keeps the statement that in the time of the city of the birth, the messenger or the uncle of the messenger who was the protector of the messenger put the guarding of the messenger in the hands of the others, and the guarding is the act that the report names as the form of the protection that the people did for the messenger. With the revealing of the verse and the promise of the protection that the verse carries, the fear left the messenger in the form of the complete leaving, and the messenger gave up the keeping of the guards, and the giving up is the act that the report keeps in the form of the statement of the change of the practice. The other of the accounts joins the assurance to the incident of the one of the desert, who took the way of the killing of the messenger and came upon the messenger with the drawn sword in the state where the messenger was alone and without the weapon, and the one put the question of the one who can guard the messenger from him, and the messenger answered with the name of God, and the hand of the one took the state of the trembling with the fear and the sword left the hand and fell, and the incident is the act that the account keeps in the form of the parallel to the account of the verse of the hand that is stretched against the messenger in the beginning of the sūrah.

**The Connection of the Verse with the Announcement at the Well**

The commentaries of the one party of the community connect this verse explicitly to the announcement of the messenger at the place of the well, which was after the completion of the pilgrimage of the farewell in the tenth year of the migration, and the announcement is the announcement regarding the cousin and the son-in-law of the messenger. The announcement is the statement that for the one for whom the messenger is the master, the cousin is the master, and the prayer to God to be the friend of the friend of the cousin and the enemy of the enemy of the cousin, and the statement is the act that the party takes as the matter of the inspiration of God that is not in the Book. The commentaries of the party suggest that the messenger was the hesitant to make the announcement because he feared the reaction of some of the companions, and the hesitating is the act that the commentaries name as the form of the state of the messenger. The verse was then revealed to the messenger, and the revealing is the act that states the duty of the messenger to convey the message of God and the promise of the protection of God for him, and after the revealing the messenger stood with the cousin in front of the gathered companions and made the announcement, and the standing and the making are the acts that the account of the party keeps in the form of the statement.

**The Connection of the Other Party and the Plausibility of the Context**

A small number of the writers of the other party also connect this verse to the statements of the messenger that relate to the spiritual merits of the cousin, including the statement of the mastery of the cousin, or to the place of the well specifically, and the connecting is the act that the writers do in the form of the reading. The other of the writers of the party connect this verse to the announcements that the messenger made during the sermon of the pilgrimage of the farewell, which preceded the event of the well by the matter of the days, and the connecting is the act that the writers do in the form of the reading that the verse pertains to the directives that the messenger gave to the community at the very end of the life of the messenger. The reports that put the revealing of this verse in the occasion of the pilgrimage of the farewell and the events that stand with it, the announcement of the well included, are the reports that the account weighs as the most plausible, and the weighing is the act that the account does in the form of the judgment on the reports, since the sūrah is the sūrah that the scholars take as the late one in the city of the refuge in its whole, and a number of the scholars hold that the opening verses of the sūrah took their revealing in the occasion of the sermon that the messenger gave in the pilgrimage of the farewell, and the holding is the act that the scholars do in the form of the support of the placing of the verse in the occasion of the farewell.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:68
> Say, ˹O Prophet,˺ “O People of the Book! You have nothing to stand on unless you observe the Torah, the Gospel, and what has been revealed to you from your Lord.” And your Lord’s revelation to you ˹O Prophet˺ will only cause many of them to increase in wickedness and disbelief. So do not grieve for the people who disbelieve.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Reiteration of the Observing in the Close**

The verse reiterates the importance of the two communities observing the three things, and the three things are their own two books, which are the book of the law and the book of the gospel, and the thing that was sent down to them from the Lord. The thing that was sent down to them is, in the understanding of the most of the commentators, the message of the Book that the messenger brought, and the understanding is the one that the verse of the nourishment before states in the same form, since the verse of the nourishment keeps the three things in the form of the condition of the nourishment, and this verse keeps the three things in the form of the condition of the standing. The reiteration is the act that the sūrah does in the verse of the close of the account of the community of the book, and the doing is the act that the sūrah does so that the command of the observing stands in the sūrah in the form of the command that is given at the beginning of the account of the judgment and at the close of the account of the charge.

**The Standing on the Naught**

The verse states the state of the two communities in the form of the standing on the naught, and the standing on the naught is the state that the communities are in in the form of the not having the ground to stand on. The ground that the verse names is the ground of the observing of the three things, and the observing is the act that the communities are to do in order to take the standing in the form of the standing on the ground. The form of the verse is the form of the address to the communities in the second person, and the addressing is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the saying, since the saying of the address is the act that the messenger does in the form of the conveying of the message to the communities, and the conveying is the act that the command of the verse before orders the messenger to do.

**The Increase in the Rebellion**

The verse states the fact that the thing that was sent down to the messenger from the Lord will increase many of the communities in the rebellion and the disbelief, and the stating is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the echo of the verse of the charge before, since the verse of the charge states the same fact in the form of the increase of the some of the communities in the rebellion and the disbelief. The echo is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the repetition of the statement, and the repetition is the act that the sūrah does so that the fact of the increase stands in the sūrah in the form of the fact that is stated in the verse of the middle of the account of the community of the book and in the verse of the close of the account, and the two together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the state of the communities in the matter of the revelation.

**The Command of the Not Grieving**

The verse closes with the command of the not grieving for the disbelieving people, and the command is the command that the Book states for the messenger in the several verses where the messenger is told not to grieve for the ones who are not the responsive to the message of God. The verse of the command of the not grieving in this sūrah is the verse that states the command in the account of the forty years of the wandering, and the verse of the sūrah of the story of the prophet who was sent to the people of the city is the verse that states the command in the form of the not being distressed by the schemes of the ones, and the two verses together are the form that the Book keeps for the command of the not grieving in the form of the address to the messenger. The command of the not grieving is the command that the sūrah does in the form of the close of the verse, and the doing is the act that the sūrah does so that the state of the messenger in the matter of the not responding of the communities is the state of the not grieving and not the state of the grief.

**The Connection with the Command of the Conveying**

Some of the commentators connect this verse to the command of the messenger in the verse before to convey the thing that was sent down to him, and the connecting is the act that the commentators do in the form of the reading. In the form of the reading, the message to the communities of the book to follow their own books as well as the Book of the sūrah is the message that the messenger was the hesitant to convey, or the message that the messenger feared to convey, and the hesitating and the fearing are the acts that the reading names as the form of the state of the messenger. The reading is the act that the commentators do in the form of the joining of the two verses, and the joining is the act that the account of the two verses does in the form of the structure, since the structure of the two verses is the structure of the command of the conveying and the statement of the message that was conveyed, and the two together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the account of the message of the command of the observing.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:69
> Indeed, the believers, Jews, Sabians and Christians—whoever ˹truly˺ believes in Allah and the Last Day and does good, there will be no fear for them, nor will they grieve.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Verse That Stands Near the Identical Verse**

The verse is the verse that stands near the identical verse of the sūrah of the family, and the identity is the identity that the two verses keep in the form of the statement of the belief and the doing of the right. The verse of the sūrah of the family states the same statement in the form of the believers, the Jews, the Christians, and the Sabians, and the stating is the act that the sūrah of the family does in the same form as the sūrah of the table spread does. The verse of the sūrah of the story of the night journey states the similar statement in the form of the judgment of God between the believers, the Jews, the Sabians, the Christians, and the people of the fire, on the day of the resurrection, and the stating is the act that the sūrah of the story does in the form of the naming of the communities that are the named in the judgment of God.

**The Affirmation in the Section of the Charge**

The verse stands in the sūrah that is largely devoted to the community of the book, and the sūrah is the sūrah that carries the long section that discusses the community of the book in the form of the charge, and the standing of the verse in the section is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the placing of the affirmation in the middle of the charge. The verse represents one of the most important statements of the Book in the form of the affirmation of the potential of the ones who are outside the community of the sūrah to achieve the salvation, and the affirmation is the act that the verse does in the form of the statement. Although the three communities of the Jews, the Christians, and the Sabians are the named in the verse, the verse refers more broadly to the one who believes in God and the last day and does the right, and the referring is the act that the verse does in the form of the opening of the possibility of the salvation even beyond the faiths of the one who was the prophet of the people of the book, since the form of the one who believes and does the right is the form that the verse keeps for the community that is wider than the three communities that are the named.

**The Three Verses That Argue for the Validity**

This verse, together with the verse of the marriage that allows the intimate relation between the men of the community of the sūrah and the believing women of the community of the book, and the verse of the law and the way that indicates that God appointed more than one of the religious laws and the ways, is the verse that argues for the continued validity of the three religions of the law, the gospel, and the faith that came after, and the arguing is the act that the three verses do in the form of the structure. The three verses together are the structure that the sūrah gives to the matter of the relation of the community of the sūrah and the community of the book, and the structure is the structure that stands against the strong criticism of the certain doctrines and the practices of the two communities that is found in this sūrah and in the other sūrahs of the Book, and the standing against is the act that the three verses do in the form of the balance, since the balance of the criticism and the affirmation is the form that the sūrah keeps for the account of the community of the book.

**The No Fear and the No Grief**

The verse states the two states of the one who believes and does the right in the form of the no fear and the no grief, and the two states are the states that the verse keeps for the one who achieves the salvation. The no fear is the no having the dread for the future after the resurrection, and the dread is the state that the verse removes from the one in the form of the statement. The no grief is the no having the sorrow about the past or about the leaving of the earthly realm, and the sorrow is the state that the verse removes from the one in the form of the statement, since what the one is certain to receive in the next world is the better than the thing that the one leaves, and the better is the act that the reading states in the form of the reason of the no grief. The two states together are the form that the verse keeps for the state of the one who has achieved the salvation, and the form is the form that indicates that such a one has no concern for the future or for the past, and the not having the concern is the act that the state of the salvation does in the form of the living of the one in the now that is the eternal of the state of the garden.

**The Verse in the Account of the Sūrah**

The sūrah keeps this verse in the place where it stands after the account of the charge of the community of the book and after the command of the conveying and the message of the observing, and the keeping is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the placing of the affirmation at the point where the account of the charge turns to the account of the covenant. The account of the sūrah runs from the charge of the state of the community to the affirmation of the potential of the salvation of the community, and the running is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the movement, since the movement of the sūrah is the movement from the telling of the state to the telling of the potential, and the two together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the account of the community of the book, which is the form of the charge and the affirmation held together in the one sūrah.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:70
> Indeed, We took a covenant from the Children of Israel and sent them messengers. Whenever a messenger came to them with what they did not desire, they denied some and killed others.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Covenant and the Messengers in the Statement**

The verse states the two acts of God for the people of the book of the law in the form of the taking of the covenant and the sending of the messengers, and the two acts are the acts that God does in the form of the address to the people. The verse is one of the several verses of the Book that mention the covenant of God with the people of the law, and the mentioning is the act that the Book does in the form of the repetition of the statement, since the statement of the covenant is the statement that the Book keeps for the relation of God and the people of the law in the form of the several verses that name the covenant in the sūrahs of the family and the table spread and the story of the one who claimed the lordship. The Book mentions the covenant of God with the community of the book in general or with the community of the gospel in the form of the several verses that name the covenant in the sūrahs of the family and the table spread and the story of the one who claimed the lordship, and the mentioning is the act that the Book does in the form of the extension of the statement of the covenant to the two communities.

**The Messenger Who Brought What the Soul Did Not Love**

The verse states the act of the messenger in the form of the bringing of the thing that the soul of the people did not love, and the bringing is the act that the messenger does in the form of the conveying of the message to the people. The form of the verse is the form of the condition of the time, which is the form of the every time that the messenger came, and the form is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the naming of the repetition of the act, since the repetition of the act of the messenger in the form of the bringing of the thing that the soul did not love is the repetition that the verse states in the form of the pattern, and the pattern is the pattern that the sūrah keeps for the relation of the messenger and the people of the law. The thing that the soul did not love is the thing that the message of the messenger carries in the form of the requirement that the soul of the people does not take, and the not taking is the act that the soul does in the form of the state that the verse names in the form of the not loving.

**The Two Acts of the People**

The verse states the two acts of the people in the form of the denying and the killing, and the two acts are the acts that the two parties of the people do toward the messenger. The first of the acts is the act of the denying of the messenger by the party of the people, and the denying is the act that the party does in the form of the refusal of the message. The second of the acts is the act of the killing of the messenger by the party of the people, and the killing is the act that the party does in the form of the violence against the messenger. The rejection of the previous messengers and the prophets by their people is the theme that runs through the whole of the Book, and the running is the act that the Book does in the form of the repetition of the account, since the account of the rejection and the killing of the certain prophets by the people of the law is the account that the Book keeps in the several verses of the sūrahs of the family and the table spread and the story of the one who claimed the lordship, in the form of the statement that the people became arrogant and rejected some of the messengers and killed the others.

**The Pattern That the Sūrah Keeps**

The sūrah keeps the verse in the form of the pattern of the four acts, and the four acts are the act of the taking of the covenant, the act of the sending of the messengers, the act of the bringing of the message, and the act of the denying and the killing of the messenger. The pattern is the pattern that the sūrah gives to the account of the people of the law in the form of the sequence of the acts, and the sequence is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the telling of the history of the people of the law. The telling of the history in the form of the pattern is the act that the sūrah does so that the community that hears takes the pattern as the pattern of the state of the people who are given the covenant and the messengers, since the pattern of the covenant and the sending and the bringing and the rejecting is the pattern that the state of the people of the book takes in the form that the sūrah names, and the naming is the act that the verse does in the form of the statement of the two acts of the people.

**The Verse in the Section of the Account**

The verse stands in the sūrah in the section that runs from the affirmation of the potential of the salvation to the charge of the belief in the divinity of the prophet of the gospel, and the standing is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the placing of the account of the covenant in the middle of the section. The verse of the affirmation before is the verse that states the potential of the salvation of the three communities in the form of the belief and the doing of the right, and the verse of the charge after is the verse that states the disbelief of the ones who say that God is the prophet of the gospel, and this verse is the verse that tells the account of the covenant and the messengers and the rejecting in the form of the history of the people of the law, and the three together are the structure that the sūrah gives to the section of the account of the community of the book, which is the structure of the affirmation, the history, and the charge of the belief.

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:71
> They thought there would be no consequences, so they turned a blind eye and a deaf ear. Yet Allah turned to them in forgiveness ˹after their repentance˺, but again many became blind and deaf. And Allah is All-Seeing of what they do.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Blindness and the Deafness of the Heart**

The verse states the state of the people in the form of the blindness and the deafness, and the state is the state of the spiritual insensitivity to the messages of the prophets. The Book refers to the spiritual insensitivity to the prophetic messages in the form of the blindness and the deafness in the several verses, and the referring is the act that the Book does in the form of the repetition of the image, since the image of the blindness and the deafness is the image that the Book keeps for the state of the one who does not take the message of the prophet in the form of the hearing and the seeing. The verse of the sūrah of the story of the camel states that it is not the eyes that are the blind, but the hearts within the breasts that are the blind, and the stating is the act that the sūrah of the story does in the form of the naming of the seat of the blindness, since the seat of the blindness is the seat of the heart and not the seat of the eye, and the naming of the seat is the act that the Book does in the form of the statement of the state of the one who is the blind in the sense of the spirit.

**The Blindness That Continues in the Next World**

The Book states that the spiritual blindness is the blindness that continues in the next world, and the continuing is the act that the state of the blindness does in the form of the carrying over of the state from this world to the next. The verse of the sūrah of the story of the people of the city states that the one who is the blind in this world is the blind in the next world and is farther astray from the right way, and the stating is the act that the sūrah of the story does in the form of the statement of the continuation. The verse of the day of the gathering of the wicked states the state of the blindness in the form of the gathering of the ones who are the blind in the face that is the blue from the horror, and the stating is the act that the sūrah of the story does in the form of the image of the face, since the image of the face is the image that the Book gives to the state of the one who is the blind in the day of the reckoning.

**The Two Sources of the State**

The Book asserts the two sources of the state of the blindness, and the two sources are the source of the preexisting moral blindness and the source of the willful act that leads to the state. The first of the sources is the source of the moral blindness that is the preexisting in the one, and the blindness is the blindness that impedes the ability of the one to be moved and convinced by the messages of the prophets in the form of the state of the heart. The second of the sources is the source of the willful mocking, the rejection, or the transgression of the messages, and the acts are the acts that lead the one to the state of the blindness in the form of the deed of the one against the message. The two sources together are the form that the Book keeps for the state of the blindness, since the state of the one who is the blind is the state that the preexisting state and the act of the will both do in the form of the bringing about of the blindness.

**The State of the Origin and the Deformity**

The Book indicates that all of the human beings are born in the state of the pure and the primordial nature from God, and the nature is the nature that the verse of the steadfastness in the faith in the uprightness names in the form of the natural way of God that God instilled in all of the people. The Book indicates that the human being has the innate awareness of the spiritual truth or at least of the lordship of God in the form of the testimony that the Lord took from the descendants of the first of the people, and the taking is the act that the sūrah of the story does in the form of the statement of the awareness. Many of the commentators have understood the blindness to the truth that the prophets bring as the result of a certain moral deformity that is brought about by the sin and the repeated moral transgression itself, or as the result of a curse of God that is the punishment for the sin, and the understanding is the act that the commentators do in the form of the reading, since the reading of the deformity and the curse is the reading that the state of the origin gives, and the verse of the condemnation of the ones that God deafened and blinded is the verse that the commentators take in the form of the reference to the state of the curse.

**The Relenting and the Returning to the State**

The verse states the two acts of the state in the form of the relenting of God and the returning of the people to the state of the blindness and the deafness. The relenting is the act that God does toward the people in the form of the turning to them, and the turning is the act that the sūrah states in the form of the statement of the mercy of God for the people of the law. The returning is the act that the many of the people do in the form of the coming back to the state of the blindness and the deafness after the relenting, and the coming back is the act that the verse states in the form of the again that the verse uses for the state. The two acts together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the cycle of the state of the people, and the cycle is the cycle of the relenting and the returning that the sūrah names in the form of the pattern of the state. The verse closes with the statement that God sees all that the people do, and the seeing is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the statement of the knowledge of God for the acts of the people, since the statement of the seeing is the statement that the sūrah keeps for the state of the one who is in the state of the blindness and the deafness, which is the state of the one whose acts are the acts that the seeing of God reaches.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:72
> Those who say, “Allah is the Messiah, son of Mary,” have certainly fallen into disbelief. The Messiah ˹himself˺ said, “O Children of Israel! Worship Allah—my Lord and your Lord.” Whoever associates others with Allah ˹in worship˺ will surely be forbidden Paradise by Allah. Their home will be the Fire. And the wrongdoers will have no helpers.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Belief That the Verse Names as the Disbelief**

The verse opens with the statement of the disbelief of the ones who say that God is the prophet of the gospel, and the statement is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the naming of the belief as the disbelief. The specific belief that God is the prophet is the belief that the sūrah has already criticized as the disbelief in the verse of the address to the community of the book, and the criticism is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the statement of the belief that is the disbelief. The refutation of the divinity of the prophet of the gospel is the refutation that the Book keeps in the several verses where the divinity of the prophet is directly or indirectly refuted, and the refuting is the act that the Book does in the form of the repetition of the statement, since the statement of the refutation is the statement that the Book keeps for the belief in the divinity of the prophet in the sūrahs of the family, the table spread, the story of the night journey, and this sūrah.

**The Words of the Prophet as the Refutation**

The verse states the words of the prophet of the gospel in the form of the refutation of the belief in the divinity of the prophet, and the words are the words that the sūrah cites in the form of the command to the people of the law to worship God in the name of the Lord of the prophet and the Lord of the people. The statement of the prophet that God is the Lord of the prophet and the Lord of the people is the statement that the Book states in the several verses where the prophet is named as the one who makes the statement, and the stating is the act that the Book does in the form of the repetition of the words of the prophet, since the words of the prophet are the words that the Book keeps for the refutation of the belief in the divinity of the prophet in the sūrahs of the family, this sūrah, the story of the birth, and the sūrah of the story of the one who claimed the lordship. From the perspective of the religion of the sūrah, the statement of the prophet asserts the full humanity of the prophet and the state of the servanthood of the prophet in the relation of the prophet to God, and the asserting is the act that the statement does in the form of the refutation, since the refutation of the divinity of the prophet is the refutation that the statement of the servanthood does in the form of the naming of the state of the prophet.

**The Parallel in the Book of the Gospel and the Problem of the Father**

The only parallel in the book of the gospel to the words that are attributed to the prophet in this verse is found in the passage where the prophet refers to God as the father of the prophet and the father of the one who speaks to the prophet, and the finding is the act that the account does in the form of the comparison of the two texts. The parallel is not the parallel that the context of the command to worship keeps, since the passage of the book of the gospel is not the passage that is in the context of the command to worship God as the verse of the Book states it, and the not keeping is the act that the account states in the form of the difference of the two contexts. The conception of God as the father is the conception that is the problematic from the perspective of the Book, and the problematic is the act that the Book does in the form of the criticism of the claim of the two communities to be the children of God in the verse of the address to the two communities, since the claim of the children of God is the claim that the Book criticizes in the form of the question of the punishment for the sins, and the criticism of the claim is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the statement.

**The Warning Against the Ascribing of the Partners**

The verse and the verse of the sūrah of the emulation where the community of the book is named as the one who takes the scholars and the monks and the prophet as the lords besides God are the verses where the criticism of the belief in the divinity of the prophet is followed by the strong warning against the ascribing of the partners to God, and the following is the act that the two verses do in the form of the structure. Some of the commentators consider any explicit claim that the prophet or the messiah is God himself to be a form of the ascribing of the partners, and the considering is the act that the commentators do in the form of the reading, and the warning of the verses and the consequences of the warning, which is the forbidding of the garden, are the warning and the consequences that are directed at the ones who make the claim in the form of the reading. Some of the major commentators indicate that the explicit claim that is criticized in this verse is the claim that is made only by the certain sects among the community of the gospel, and the indicating is the act that the commentators do in the form of the naming of the sects, and the sects are the sects that rejected the notion of the dual nature of the prophet as the human and the divine and instead asserted the more essential unity between God and the prophet, and the asserting is the act that the sects do in the form of the doctrine that the verse of the charge names.

**The Closing of the Charge**

The verse closes with the statement of the state of the one who ascribes the partners to God in the form of the forbidding of the garden and the naming of the fire as the abode of the one, and the two statements are the statements that the sūrah keeps for the close of the charge of the belief. The forbidding of the garden is the act that God does to the one in the form of the not admitting of the one to the garden, and the naming of the fire is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the statement of the abode of the one, and the two together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the state of the one who ascribes the partners in the next world. The statement that the wrongdoers shall have no helpers is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the close of the verse, and the close is the act that the sūrah does so that the state of the one who ascribes the partners is the state of the one who has no helper in the matter of the belief that the verse charges, since the charge of the belief in the divinity of the prophet is the charge that the sūrah states in the form of the refutation and the warning and the closing together.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:73
> Those who say, “Allah is one in a Trinity,” have certainly fallen into disbelief. There is only One God. If they do not stop saying this, those who disbelieve among them will be afflicted with a painful punishment.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Doctrine the Verse Takes in the Charge**

The verse takes in the charge the doctrine that stands in the place of the doctrine of the nature of the prophet in the verse before, and the doctrine is the doctrine of the three in the form of the statement that God is the third of the three. The charge of this verse is the charge of the doctrine that is the doctrine of the three entities that are the separate and that are to be the worshipped, and the doctrine is the doctrine that the sūrah takes in the form of the exaggerated form of the teaching of the three that the community of the gospel carries, since the sūrah charges the doctrine in the form of the statement that God is the one of the three, and the statement is the statement that the verse keeps for the doctrine in the form of the charge of the disbelief.

**The Three That the Commentators Name**

Some of the commentators take the three that the verse charges as the three that are God, the prophet of the gospel, and the mother of the prophet, and the taking is the act that the two commentators do in the form of the reading of the three as the three distinct deities. The reading rests on the verse of the humanity of the prophet and the mother that this sūrah states in the verse after, and on the verse of this sūrah that takes the divinization of the mother among some of the followers of the prophet in the form of the direct criticism, and the two verses together are the ground that the reading of the three keeps in the sūrah. The commentators were generally aware that the orthodox teaching of the three of the community of the gospel considers the three as the three persons in the single divinity, which are God, the one who was the prophet, and the spirit, and not the mother, and the awareness is the act that the commentators do in the form of the knowledge of the teaching. Some of the commentators argue that the belief in the three distinct deities is the belief of the certain sects of the community, which are the sects that are named in the reading, and the arguing is the act that the commentators do in the form of the limiting of the charge to the sects. One of the later commentators takes the three persons of the orthodox teaching as the three aspects of God for the community of the gospel, which are the aspect of the essence, the aspect of the word, and the aspect of the life, and the taking is the act that the commentator does in the form of the explanation of the teaching in the terms that make the teaching the thing that is the meaningful in the relation of the view of the religion of the sūrah to the essence of God and the attributes of God, although the explanation is not the explanation that moves the commentator to take the teaching as the acceptable, and the commentator describes the teaching as the clear error in the form of the charge.

**The Refraining That the Command Names**

The sūrah of the family commands the community of the book in the verse of the charge of the three to hold back from the saying of the three, and the saying is the saying that is commonly understood in the reference to the teaching of the three, and the command is the act that the sūrah of the family does in the form of the holding back from the word. The present verse takes the saying that God is the third of the three in the form of the clear identification of the saying as the form of the disbelief, and the identification is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the naming of the state of the one who says. The threat of the painful punishment is the threat that the sūrah keeps for the ones who do not hold back from the asserting of the saying, and the threat is the threat that stands in the place of the command of the holding back that the sūrah of the family did in the form of the command to the community of the book, since the command of the holding back and the threat of the punishment are the two forms that the two sūrahs keep for the matter of the saying of the three.

**The Measure of the Threat in the Readings**

Some of the commentators argue that the threat of the punishment is not the threat that is limited to the certain sects of the community of the gospel, but the threat that is the threat for the community of the gospel in the whole, and the arguing is the act that the two commentators do in the form of the reading, since the Book suggests in this verse and in the other verses that the certain common teachings of the community are the forms of the disbelief, and the punishment of this verse is the punishment that is threatened for all of the ones who disbelieve, or at least for the ones who persist in this disbelief and do not turn to God in the repentance, and the turning is the act that the verse after urges them to do. The reading is not the reading that the form of the verse supports, since the verse clearly threatens the punishment only for the ones among the community who disbelieved, and the limiting of the threat to the ones who disbelieved is the act that the verse does in the form of the naming of the state. The reading that bars all of the community of the gospel from the garden in the next life is the reading that stands openly against the verse of the potential of the salvation of the community of the book that this sūrah states, and against the identical verse of the sūrah of the family, and against the verse of the upright among the community of the book that the sūrah of the family names, and the standing against is the act that the reading does in the form of the contradiction, since the three verses together are the form that the Book keeps for the state of the one who believes and does the right in the community of the book. The reading is also the reading that does not stand with the description of the virtue of the community of the gospel that this sūrah states in the verses of the account of the listening of the revelation, and the description is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the account of the grace of the community.

**The Verse and the Verse of the Turning**

The sūrah keeps this verse and the verse after in the form of the threat and the opening, and the two together are the structure that the sūrah gives to the account of the doctrine of the three. The verse of this charge states the threat of the punishment for the state of the one who does not hold back from the saying of the three, and the verse after states the opening of the repentance and the seeking of the forgiveness for the one who is in the state of the doctrine that is against the oneness of God, and the two together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the account of the doctrine, which is the form of the charge of the state and the invitation of the turning held together in the two verses.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:74
> Will they not turn to Allah in repentance and seek His forgiveness? And Allah is All-Forgiving, Most Merciful.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Invitation That the Verse Keeps**

The verse keeps the invitation of the repentance for the ones who hold the doctrines of the nature of the prophet and the doctrine of the three that stand against the principle of the oneness of God, and the keeping is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the address of the state. The invitation is the act that the sūrah does to the ones who are in the state of the doctrine, and the state is the state that the verse before named in the form of the charge of the disbelief, and the inviting is the act that the sūrah does in the verse after the charge, since the charge of the verse before is the charge that leaves the one in the state of the threat, and the invitation of this verse is the invitation that the sūrah does to take the one out of the state of the threat in the form of the turning.

**The Two Acts of the Return**

The verse names the two acts of the return in the form of the turning and the seeking, and the two acts are the acts that the one is to do in the state of the repentance. The turning is the act of the heart that turns from the doctrine to God in the form of the leaving of the state, and the seeking is the act of the one who seeks the forgiveness of God in the form of the asking of the pardon, and the two together are the form that the verse keeps for the repentance, since the repentance is the act of the turning and the asking held together in the one act of the return. The verse states the two acts in the form of the question, and the question is the form that the sūrah does in the act of the inviting, since the form of the question is the form that the sūrah uses for the act of the one who does not do the act that the sūrah commands, and the not doing is the state that the question names in the form of the wonder of the sūrah.

**The Two Names of God at the Close**

The verse closes with the two names of God in the form of the forgiving and the merciful, and the two names are the names that the sūrah keeps for the close of the invitation. The name of the forgiving is the name that the verse gives to God in the form of the act of the pardoning of the sin of the one who turns, and the name of the merciful is the name that the verse gives to God in the form of the act of the carrying of the state of the one who seeks, and the two together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the ground of the invitation, since the ground of the invitation is the ground of the act of God that the two names name, and the act of the pardoning and the act of the carrying are the acts that the one who repents takes as the acts that the state of the turning produces in the next act of God.

**The Verse as the Answer to the Threat**

The verse stands in the sūrah in the place where it answers the threat of the verse before, and the answering is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the placing of the opening after the charge. The verse before is the verse that states the threat of the painful punishment for the ones who do not hold back from the saying of the three, and this verse is the verse that states the opening of the repentance and the forgiveness for the ones who are in the state of the doctrine, and the two together are the structure that the sūrah gives to the account of the doctrine of the three, which is the structure of the threat and the opening held together in the two verses. The placing of the opening after the threat is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the mercy, and the mercy is the form that the sūrah keeps for the close of the account of the doctrine, since the account of the doctrine is the account that ends in the invitation and not in the threat, and the invitation is the act that the sūrah does to the community that hears in the form of the opening of the state of the one who is in the doctrine.

**The Persistence That the Question Names**

The question of the verse is the question that names the state of the persistence of the ones in the doctrine, and the persistence is the state that the question takes in the form of the wonder of the sūrah at the not turning of the ones. The form of the question is the form that the Book uses for the state of the one who does not do the act that the Book commands, and the not doing is the state that the question names in the form of the act that the sūrah expects of the one and does not find in the state of the one. The sūrah keeps the question in the verse of the invitation, and the keeping is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the address of the state of the persistence, since the state of the one who persists in the doctrine against the oneness of God is the state that the question of the verse names in the form of the thing that is the thing that the sūrah does not expect of the one who is in the state of the belief, and the not expecting is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the charge of the persistence.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:75
> The Messiah, son of Mary, was no more than a messenger. ˹Many˺ messengers had ˹come and˺ gone before him. His mother was a woman of truth. They both ate food. See how We make the signs clear to them, yet see how they are deluded ˹from the truth˺!

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Messenger in the Form of the Refutation**

The verse states the prophet of the gospel in the form of the messenger only, and the stating is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the refutation of the claim of the divinity of the prophet. The prophet is described as the messenger of God throughout the Book, and the describing is the act that the Book does in the form of the repetition of the naming of the state of the prophet. The sūrah of the family describes the prophet as the messenger only in the verse of the charge of the divinity, and the describing is the act that the sūrah of the family does in the same form as this sūrah does, although the sūrah of the family identifies the prophet in the same verse as the word of God and the spirit from God, and the identifying is the act that the sūrah of the family does in the form of the naming of the two states of the prophet that stand with the state of the messenger. The Book states the identical statement concerning the messenger of the sūrah in the verse of the sūrah of the family where the statement that the messenger is the messenger only and that the messengers have passed on before him is the statement that the sūrah of the family keeps for the prophet of the sūrah, and the stating is the act that the Book does in the form of the parallel of the two statements, since the parallel of the two statements is the act that the Book does in the form of the naming of the state of the two messengers in the one form.

**The Mother Who Is the Woman of Truth**

The verse describes the mother of the prophet in the form of the woman of the truth, and the description is the description that the sūrah keeps for the state of the mother in the form of the feminine form of the word that the Book uses for the prophet and the saintly people. The word of the truth is the word that the Book uses for the man of the truth in the verse where the father of the prophets is named as the man of the truth and the prophet, and in the verse where the prophet who was raised is named in the same form, and the using is the act that the Book does in the form of the naming of the state of the truthful in the account of the prophets. The verse of the company of the blessed names the truthful in the company of the prophets, the witnesses, and the righteous, and the naming is the act that the Book does in the form of the placing of the truthful in the company of God. Some of the commentators connect the description of the mother as the woman of the truth to her role as the one who affirmed the truth of the prophethood of the prophet and the message of the prophet, and the connecting is the act that the commentator does in the form of the parallel to the companion of the messenger who was given the title of the truthful because he affirmed the truth of the night journey of the messenger, and the parallel is the act that the commentator does in the form of the joining of the two states. The other of the commentators connect the description to the statement of the sūrah of the belief where the mother is named as the one who testified to the words of her Lord and His books, and the connecting is the act that the commentator does in the form of the reference to the state of the testifying of the mother.

**The Eating of the Food in the Affirmation**

The verse states the act of the eating of the food by the prophet and the mother in the form of the affirmation of the full humanity of the two, and the affirmation is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the refutation of the ones who see the two as the divine. The act of the eating is the act of the body that needs the food, and the needing of the food is the state of the body that is the state of the human being, and the sūrah states the state in the form of the charge of the belief in the divinity of the two, since the belief in the divinity of the one who eats is the belief that the sūrah takes in the form of the charge in the verse of the account of the two. The teaching of the community of the gospel also holds the prophet as the fully human in the form that it holds him as the fully divine, and the holding is the act that the teaching does in the form of the two states of the prophet. The view of the Book of the prophet as the fully human is the view that stands with the certain passages of the book of the gospel that stress the humanity of the prophet in the relation of the prophet to God, and the standing is the act that the account does in the form of the comparison of the two texts.

**The Making Clear of the Signs**

The verse states the act of God in the form of the making clear of the signs to the ones, and the making clear is the act that God does in the form of the putting of the signs in the state of the clearness for the ones. The signs that the verse names are the signs of this verse and the signs of the verses around it, which are the signs of the state of the prophet as the messenger and the signs of the state of the mother as the woman of the truth and the signs of the state of the two as the ones who eat the food, and the three together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the signs that God makes clear to the ones in the account of the prophet and the mother. The seeing of the making clear is the act that the sūrah commands in the form of the address of the community that hears, and the commanding is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the invitation to the taking of the signs in the state of the clearness, since the taking of the signs in the state of the clearness is the act that the community is to do in the form of the understanding of the account of the prophet and the mother.

**The Perverting of the Ones**

The verse closes with the statement of the state of the ones in the form of the perverting, and the perverting is the act that the ones do in the form of the turning of the signs from the state of the clearness to the state of the astray. The statement of the perverting is the statement that the sūrah keeps for the close of the verse in the form of the charge, and the charge is the charge of the state of the one who does not take the signs in the state of the clearness that God put them in, since the state of the one who perverts the signs is the state that the verse names in the form of the thing that is the opposite of the state of the making clear, and the opposite is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the contrast of the act of God and the act of the ones, which is the contrast of the putting of the signs in the clearness and the turning of the signs from the clearness.

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:76
> Say, ˹O Prophet,˺ “How can you worship besides Allah those who can neither harm nor benefit you? And Allah ˹alone˺ is the All-Hearing, AllKnowing.”

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Question That the Book Keeps in the Several Verses**

The Book poses the question of this verse in the form of the rhetorical question in the several verses, either in the direct form or in the form of the words of the prophets, and the posing is the act that the Book does in the form of the repetition of the question as the argument against the ones who worship the thing other than God. The verses that carry the question are the verses of the sūrahs that name the worship of the thing that cannot benefit or harm in the form of the charge, and the naming is the act that the Book does in the form of the repetition of the statement, since the statement of the worship of the thing that has no power of the benefit or the harm is the statement that the Book keeps for the charge of the worship in the sūrah of the story of the judgment and the sūrah of the thunder and the sūrah of the story of the first of the prophets and the sūrah of the criterion and the sūrah of the story of the gathering and the sūrah of the story of the light and the sūrah of the story of the letters, in the form of the question and the statement together.

**The Object That Has No Power**

The verse names the object of the worship in the form of the thing that has no power of the benefit or the harm for the ones who worship it, and the naming is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the description of the object. The description of the object as the thing that cannot benefit or harm is the description that the sūrah keeps for the object of the worship in the form of the charge, since the charge of the worship of the object is the charge that the description of the object does in the form of the statement of the absence of the power of the object, and the absence of the power is the state that the object is in in the form of the thing that the worship does not give it. The question of the verse is the question that puts the worship of the object in the form of the thing that the one does not do in the state of the knowledge of the object, and the putting is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the address of the state of the one who worships the object, since the state of the one who worships the thing that has no power is the state that the question names in the form of the thing that the question does not expect of the one who has the understanding of the state of the object.

**The Two Names of God in the Answer**

The verse closes with the two names of God in the form of the hearing and the knowing, and the two names are the names that the sūrah keeps for the answer of the question. The name of the hearing is the name that the verse gives to God in the form of the act of the hearing of the worship of the one who worships, and the name of the knowing is the name that the verse gives to God in the form of the act of the knowing of the state of the one who worships the object that has no power, and the two together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the state of God in the matter of the worship, since the state of God is the state of the one who hears the worship and knows the state of the one who does the worship, and the hearing and the knowing are the acts that the sūrah names for God in the form of the answer to the question of the worship of the object.

**The Verse in the Account of the Doctrine**

The verse stands in the sūrah in the place where it comes after the verse of the humanity of the prophet and the mother and before the verse of the command of the not exaggerating, and the standing is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the placing of the question of the worship in the account of the doctrine of the community of the gospel. The verse before is the verse that states the state of the prophet and the mother in the form of the messenger and the woman of the truth and the ones who eat the food, and the verse after is the verse that commands the community of the book not to exaggerate in the religion beyond the truth, and this verse is the verse that states the question of the worship of the thing that has no power in the form of the argument against the worship of the prophet or the mother, and the three together are the structure that the sūrah gives to the account of the doctrine, which is the structure of the statement of the humanity, the question of the worship, and the command of the not exaggerating.

**The Question for the Community That Hears**

The sūrah puts the question of the verse in the account so that the community that hears takes the question as the question of the state of the one who worships the thing other than God, and the taking is the act that the sūrah expects of the reader. The question is the question that the community is to take in the form of the argument that the question carries against the worship of the object that has no power, and the argument is the argument that the sūrah keeps in the form of the question, since the form of the question is the form that the sūrah uses for the act of the taking of the state of the object in the form of the thing that the worship does not fit, and the not fitting is the act that the question does in the form of the charge of the worship. The community that hears is to take the question as the question of the state of the worship that the community does, and the taking is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the address of the state of the community in the matter of the worship of the thing that is the thing other than God.

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:77
> Say, “O People of the Book! Do not go to extremes in your faith beyond the truth, nor follow the vain desires of those who went astray before ˹you˺. They misled many and strayed from the Right Way.”

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Command of the Not Exaggerating**

The verse states the command of the not exaggerating in the religion beyond the truth for the community of the book, and the stating is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the address of the community. The sūrah of the family states the same command for the community of the book in the verse of the charge of the divinity of the prophet, and the stating is the act that the sūrah of the family does in the same form as this sūrah does, since the command of the not exaggerating is the command that the two sūrahs keep for the community of the book in the form of the holding back from the going beyond the truth in the religion. The command of both of the verses is the command that is addressed primarily to the community of the gospel in the matter of the claim of the divinity of the prophet, and the addressing is the act that the two verses do in the form of the putting of the command in the account of the doctrine of the community of the gospel, since the doctrine of the divinity of the prophet is the doctrine that the command of the not exaggerating takes in the form of the charge.

**The People Who Went Astray Before**

The people who went astray before, whom the verse commands the community not to follow in the caprices, are the people that the commentators take in the several readings, and the readings are the readings that the word of the people allows in the context of the verse. One of the readings takes the people as the people of the law, and the taking is the one that the commentator does in the form of the reading of the people as the ones who rejected the prophethood and the message of the prophet of the gospel in the whole, since the rejecting of the prophethood is the going astray of the people of the law in the form that the reading names. The other of the readings takes the people as the early leaders and the authorities of the community of the gospel who established the doctrines of the nature of the prophet and the doctrine of the three that the verse charges, and the establishing is the act that the reading names as the form of the going astray of the leaders, and the leading of the many astray is the act that the reading names as the consequence of the establishing, which is the consequence that the leaders did through the doctrines and later through the rejection of the messenger of the sūrah. The reading of the other takes the verse as the verse that is addressed to the two communities of the book together, and the addressing is the act that the reading does in the form of the putting of the command in the account of the two, and the reading urges the two to accept the truth that the prophet of the gospel was the major prophet of God and to believe in the message and the signs that the prophet brought, and not to exaggerate beyond this to the claim of the divinity of the prophet or the sonship of the prophet in the relation of the prophet to God. The reading of the fourth takes the verse as the verse that urges the two communities to avoid the various errors that they or their religious leaders made in the past, and the errors are the errors that the long section of the sūrah that begins with the verse of the case of the two fornicators discusses in the form of the account, and the avoiding is the act that the reading keeps in the form of the command of the verse.

**The Exaggeration That the Command Forbids**

The verse names the exaggeration in the form of the going beyond the truth in the religion, and the going beyond is the act that the command forbids in the form of the naming of the measure. The truth is the measure that the command keeps for the religion in the form of the limit that the one does not pass, and the limit is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the naming of the state of the religion that is the state of the truth. The exaggeration is the act of the one who puts the religion in the form that goes beyond the form of the truth, and the putting is the act that the command of the verse forbids in the form of the charge of the state of the one who does the putting. The sūrah keeps the command in the form of the address to the community of the book, and the keeping is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the saying, since the saying of the command is the act that the messenger does in the form of the conveying of the message to the community, and the conveying is the act that the command of the not exaggerating puts in the form of the duty of the messenger.

**The Caprices of the People Who Led Astray**

The verse names the state of the people who went astray before in the form of the three marks, and the three marks are the mark of the going astray of the people themselves, the mark of the leading of the many astray by the people, and the mark of the straying of the people from the right way. The three marks together are the form that the verse keeps for the state of the people in the form of the description of the people whose caprices the community is not to follow, and the description is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the naming of the state of the people. The caprice of the people is the caprice that the verse commands the community not to follow in the form of the charge of the following, and the following of the caprice is the act that the community is not to do in the form of the taking of the way of the people as the way of the community, since the way of the people who went astray and led the many astray and strayed from the right way is the way that the command of the verse names in the form of the thing that the community is not to take.

**The Verse in the Section of the Charge**

The verse stands in the sūrah in the section of the charge of the community of the book that the sūrah tells from the verse of the case of the two fornicators to the verse of the account of the listening of the revelation, and the standing is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the placing of the command of the not exaggerating in the middle of the section. The section is the section that the sūrah keeps for the account of the doctrines and the practices of the community of the book, and the command of the verse is the command that the sūrah does in the form of the putting of the limit of the truth in the account of the doctrines, since the doctrines of the community of the book are the doctrines that the command of the not exaggerating takes in the form of the charge in the section of the account, and the charge of the doctrines is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the telling of the section from the command of the judging to the command of the not exaggerating.

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:78
> The disbelievers among the Children of Israel were condemned in the revelations of David and Jesus, son of Mary. That was for their disobedience and violations.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Curse of the Two Tongues**

The verse states the state of the disbelievers among the people of the law in the form of the cursing, and the cursing is the act that the verse names in the form of the two tongues of the two prophets, which are the tongue of the prophet of the book of the praise and the tongue of the prophet of the gospel. The most of the commentators read the word of the curse in the sense of the exiling from the mercy of God, and the reading is the act that the commentators do in the form of the meaning of the word. The cursing of this verse is the cursing that is not the cursing of God, but the cursing of the two prophets, and the distinction is the act that the verse does in the form of the naming of the agents of the cursing, since the agents of the cursing are the two prophets and not God, and the naming of the agents is the act that the verse does in the form of the statement of the state of the cursing that is the state of the cursing of the prophet and not the state of the cursing of God.

**The Statements the Early Accounts Keep**

Some of the early commentators take the cursing of the two tongues as the reference to the statements that are found in the book of the praise of the first of the prophets and in the book of the gospel of the prophet of the gospel, and the statements are the statements that concern the disbelievers among the people of the law, and the referring is the act that the commentators do in the form of the reading of the cursing as the statement of the prophets in the books of the prophets. Some of the commentators mention that the disbelievers among the people of the law were also the cursed in the book of the law of the prophet of the law and in the Book that the messenger of the sūrah brought, and the mentioning is the act that the two commentators do in the form of the extension of the reading of the cursing to the two books, since the extension of the cursing to the book of the law and the Book of the sūrah is the act that the mentioning does in the form of the naming of the four books in which the cursing is the named.

**The Connections of the Cursing in the Sūrah**

Some of the commentators connect the cursing of the two tongues with the cursing of God that this sūrah states in the verse of the charge of the recompense, and the cursing is the cursing by which some of the people were made into the apes and the swine, and the connecting is the act that the commentator does in the form of the joining of the two cursings, since the joining of the cursing of the prophets and the cursing of God is the act that the account of the two verses does in the form of the structure of the account of the state of the disbelievers among the people of the law. Many of the commentators connect the cursing of the tongue of the first of the prophets with the cursing of the ones who broke the day of the rest that the sūrah of the story of the one who claimed the lordship states in the verses of the account of the people of the town by the sea, and the people are the people that the account names in the form of the reference, and the connecting is the act that the three commentators do in the form of the joining of the two accounts. The commentators connect the cursing of the tongue of the prophet of the gospel with the disbelieving of the ones after the miracle of the table that was spread with the food and sent down to the prophet, and the miracle is the miracle that this sūrah tells in the verses of the account of the asking of the table, and the connecting is the act that the three commentators do in the form of the joining of the cursing and the account of the table.

**The Disobeying and the Transgressing**

The verse states the cause of the cursing in the form of the two acts of the disbelievers among the people of the law, and the two acts are the act of the disobeying and the act of the transgressing of the limits. The disobeying is the act of the one who does not do the command of God in the form of the leaving of the obedience, and the transgressing is the act of the one who goes beyond the limits that God set in the form of the passing of the measure, and the two together are the form that the verse keeps for the state of the disbelievers in the form of the naming of the cause of the cursing. The sūrah keeps the statement of the cause in the form of the because that the verse uses for the cause, and the keeping is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the putting of the cause in the verse after the statement of the cursing, since the putting of the cause after the cursing is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the statement of the relation of the two acts of the disbelievers and the state of the cursing that the two acts produce.

**The Verse in the Account of the Sūrah**

The verse stands in the sūrah in the place where it comes after the verse of the command of the not exaggerating, and the standing is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the placing of the charge of the past of the people of the law after the command of the present of the community of the book. The command of the verse before is the command that the sūrah does in the form of the address of the community of the book in the state of the present, and this verse is the verse that states the state of the disbelievers among the people of the law in the form of the charge of the past, and the two together are the structure that the sūrah gives to the account of the community of the book, which is the structure of the command of the not exaggerating in the present and the charge of the cursing of the past held together in the two verses, and the two together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the account of the relation of the present of the community and the past of the people of the law.
