## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:79
> They did not forbid one another from doing evil. Evil indeed was what they did!

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Forbidding That the Verse Charges**

The verse states the state of the disbelievers among the people of the law in the form of the not forbidding, and the not forbidding is the act that the community does not do in the form of the leaving of the duty of the forbidding of the evil. The act of the forbidding of the evil is the act that the community is to do toward the evil that is done in the community, and the doing is the act that the verse charges in the form of the not doing of the act. The verse names the evil in the form of the thing that the community committed, and the committing is the act of the community in the form of the deed of the evil among the members of the community, and the evil is the evil that is done among themselves or by the people of the community, and the two forms together are the state that the verse charges in the form of the not forbidding.

**The Duty That the Book Keeps for the Community**

The Book mentions the importance of the forbidding of the evil in the several verses, and the mentioning is the act that the Book does in the form of the repetition of the command of the forbidding alongside the command of the enjoining of the good. The verses that carry the two commands are the verses of the sūrah of the family that describe the believers as the ones who encourage the good and forbid the evil and race in the doing of the good, and the verse of the sūrah of the emulation that describes the believers as the ones who repent and are devoted to the worship and encourage the good and forbid the evil, and the verse of the sūrah of the story of the gathering that names the establishing of the community as the community that performs the prayer and pays the alms and encourages the good and forbids the evil, and the verse of the sūrah of the story of the youth that carries the command of the father to the son in the form of the establishing of the prayer and the encouraging of the good and the forbidding of the evil. The forbidding of the wrong among the ones of the community is the duty of the religion in the law of the religion, and the duty is the duty that is the obligatory in the form of the central moral responsibility of the individual of the community and the important basis of the moral society, and the two together are the ground that the verse of the charge keeps for the not forbidding of the community of the people of the law.

**The Verse and the Verse of the Sages**

The sūrah keeps this verse and the verse of the charge of the sages in the form of the parallel of the two charges, and the parallel is the parallel of the charge of the not forbidding of the community and the charge of the not forbidding of the leaders of the community. The verse of the sages is the verse that takes the leaders of the community in the form of the charge of the not forbidding of the followers from the speech of the sin and the consuming of the unlawful, and this verse is the verse that takes the community in the form of the charge of the not forbidding of the evil that the community committed, and the two together are the structure that the sūrah gives to the matter of the forbidding of the evil in the community of the people of the law, which is the structure of the charge of the leaders and the charge of the community held together in the two verses, and the two charges together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the state of the community in the matter of the duty of the forbidding.

**The Word of Truth in the Striving**

One of the commentators puts the report of the striving of the word of truth in the form of the support of the importance of the forbidding of the evil, and the report is the report that the commentator keeps in the form of the saying that the best of the striving is the word of the truth, and the saying is the act that the report does in the form of the naming of the act of the word as the act of the striving. The putting of the report is the act that the commentator does in the form of the joining of the report to the matter of the forbidding, since the forbidding of the evil is the act that the word of the truth does in the form of the deed of the community toward the evil, and the word of the truth is the word that the report names in the form of the best of the striving of the community in the cause of the religion. The sūrah keeps the verse of the charge in the form of the statement of the not doing of the act of the word, and the stating is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the charge of the state of the community that does not do the act of the word of the truth in the matter of the evil that the community commits.

**The Closing of the Charge**

The verse closes with the statement of the evil of the doing of the community, and the statement is the statement that the sūrah keeps for the close of the charge in the form of the naming of the state of the doing. The doing is the act that the community does in the form of the committing of the evil and the not forbidding of the evil, and the two acts together are the form that the closing names in the form of the doing that is the evil. The statement of the evil of the doing is the statement that the sūrah does in the form of the parallel to the closing of the verse of the hastening to the sin and the enmity, and the parallel is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the keeping of the same form of the close in the two verses of the charge, since the two verses of the charge of the community of the people of the law are the verses that the sūrah keeps in the form of the parallel of the statement of the evil of the doing, and the parallel of the closings is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the structure of the account of the state of the community.

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:80
> You see many of them taking the disbelievers as allies. Truly wicked are their misdeeds, which have earned them Allah’s wrath. And they will be in everlasting torment.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Alliance That the Verse Sees**

The verse states the vision of the messenger for the many of the community of the book in the form of the allying with the ones who disbelieve, and the allying is the act that the many do in the form of the taking of the tie of the protection with the disbelievers. The vision is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the showing of the state of the many to the messenger, and the showing is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the seeing of the tie, since the tie of the protection with the disbelievers is the tie that the state of the many takes in the form that the sūrah shows to the messenger in the verse of the account of the community of the book. The sūrah keeps the verse in the form of the account of the alliance, and the keeping is the act that the sūrah does in the middle of the section of the charge of the community of the book, since the section of the charge is the section that the sūrah keeps for the account of the state of the community in the matter of the relation with the community of the sūrah and the relation with the disbelievers.

**The Distinction That the Two Verses Keep**

The sūrah keeps this verse and the verse after in the form of the distinction between the community of the book and the disbelievers, and the distinction is the act that the two verses do in the form of the statement of the state of the two. The community of the book is sometimes taken in the sense of the disbeliever not in the absolute form, but in the form of the not meeting the requirement of the Book of the believing in all of the prophets and the books, and the taking is the act that the reading does in the form of the limiting of the state of the disbelief to the not believing in the messenger of the sūrah and the Book of the sūrah. The two verses establish the clear distinction between the community of the book and the disbelievers, and the establishing is the act that the two verses do in the form of the criticism of the community for the allying with the disbelievers or the taking of the protectors among the disbelievers, since the criticism of the allying is the criticism that the distinction does in the form of the naming of the two communities as the two that are not the one in the state of the disbelief.

**The Forbidding of the Same Act for the Community of the Sūrah**

The Book forbids the believers of the community of the sūrah from the seeking of the protectors among the disbelievers and the wrongdoers, and the wrongdoers are the protectors of one another in the form that the sūrah of the story of the gathering names, and the protectors of each other in the form that the sūrah of the story of the letters names, and the forbidding is the act that the Book does in the form of the parallel of the two states. The Book forbids the believers from the seeking of the protectors among the two communities of the book in the verse of the prohibition of the guardianship of this sūrah, and the forbidding is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the command of the not taking of the two communities as the protectors. The two verses of this account take the community of the book in the form of the ones who do the act that the Book forbids the community of the sūrah from doing, and the taking is the act that the two verses do in the form of the charge, since the charge of the allying is the charge that the parallel of the two commands does in the form of the naming of the state of the community of the book as the state of the one who does the act that the community of the sūrah is not to do.

**The Occasion the Accounts Keep**

The two verses are understood by the commentators as the reference to the certain members of the community of the book of the city, or to the certain ones among them who are in the state of the hypocrisy, and the ones are the ones who allied with the idolaters against the believers of the community of the sūrah, and the allying is the act that the three commentators name as the form of the state of the ones. The accounts that the sūrah of the family keeps for the reported cooperation between the certain members of the community of the city and the idolaters of the city of the birth are the accounts that the sūrah of the family tells in the form of the parallel to the account of this sūrah, and the parallel is the act that the two sūrahs do in the form of the telling of the state of the relation of the community of the book and the idolaters. The sūrah keeps the two verses in the form of the account of the alliance, and the keeping is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the telling of the state of the ones who ally with the disbelievers in the middle of the section of the charge of the community of the book.

**The Closing of the Verse**

The verse closes with the three statements of the state of the ones who ally with the disbelievers, and the three statements are the statement of the evil of the thing that the ones send forth for their souls, the statement of the anger of God that is upon the ones, and the statement of the abiding of the ones in the punishment. The thing that the ones send forth for their souls is the deed of the ones in this life for which the recompense of the ones is in the next life, and the deed is the act that the verse names in the form of the sending forth of the ones for the souls in the form of the statement of the evil of the deed. The anger of God is the anger that the verse states for the ones in the form of the thing that is upon the ones, and the abiding in the punishment is the act that the verse states for the ones in the form of the state of the ones in the punishment, and the three statements together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the close of the charge of the allying, since the charge of the allying with the disbelievers is the charge that the sūrah states in the form of the evil of the deed and the anger of God and the abiding in the punishment held together in the closing of the verse.

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:81
> Had they believed in Allah, the Prophet, and what has been revealed to him, they would have never taken those ˹pagans˺ as allies. But most of them are rebellious.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Counterfactual of the Belief**

The verse opens with the statement of the condition of the belief in the three things, and the three things are God, the messenger, and the thing that was sent down to the messenger. The statement of the condition in the form of the thing that was not the act of the ones is the form that the sūrah keeps for the verse, and the keeping is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the counterfactual, since the counterfactual is the form that the sūrah gives to the state of the ones in the matter of the allying with the disbelievers. The consequence of the condition is the consequence of the not taking of the disbelievers as the protectors, and the not taking is the act that the condition of the belief carries in the form of the thing that the ones would have done if the ones had done the act of the belief, and the would have done is the form that the verse keeps for the state of the ones in the form of the thing that was not the state of the fact.

**The Two Readings of the Condition**

The condition of the belief is taken by the commentators in the two readings, and the two readings are the readings that the word of the messenger allows in the context of the verse. In the first reading, the condition is the condition of the belief in God and the messenger of the sūrah and the Book of the sūrah, and the reading is the one that the commentator gives in the form of the reference to the refusal of the ones to believe in the messenger of the sūrah and the Book of the sūrah, since the refusal of the belief in the messenger and the Book is the refusal that the condition of the verse names in the form of the thing that was not the act of the ones. In the other reading, the condition is the condition of the belief in God and the messenger of the community of the book, who is the prophet of the law, and the book that was sent down to the messenger, which is the book of the law, and the reading is the one that the commentator gives in the form of the reference to the refusal of the ones to believe in their own prophet and their own book, since the refusal of the belief in the own prophet and the own book is the refusal that the reading names in the form of the thing that was not the act of the ones.

**The Choice That the Rise of the Messenger Gave**

Both of the readings indicate that the rising of the messenger of the sūrah as the prophet put the members of the community of the book of the land in the state of the choice, and the choice is the choice of the two acts that the state of the rise carries. The first of the acts is the act of the allying with the messenger of the sūrah, and the message of the messenger is the message that both affirmed and challenged the religious understanding and the practice of the community of the book, and the affirming and the challenging are the two acts that the message does in the form of the relation of the message to the religion of the community. The second of the acts is the act of the allying with the disbelievers, which are the idolaters, and the religious view of the idolaters is the view that is the abhorrent to the religion of the community of the book, and the abhorrence is the act that the view does in the form of the state of the relation of the two religions. The two acts together are the form that the state of the rise keeps for the choice of the community of the book, and the choice is the act that the two verses of the account of the alliance do in the form of the naming of the two states of the community in the matter of the allying.

**The Deeds Sent Forth for the Souls**

The verse before states the thing that the ones send forth for their souls in the form of the evil of the deed, and the deed is the deed of the ones in this life for which the recompense of the ones is in the next life, and the recompense is the act that the verse of the sūrah of the standing keeps for the state of the ones in the form of the informing of the ones of the thing that they send forth and the thing that they leave behind, and the informing is the act that the sūrah of the standing does in the form of the statement of the reckoning of the deeds of the ones. The deed of the ones in the account of this sūrah is the deed of the allying with the disbelievers, and the allying is the act that the ones send forth for the souls in the form of the deed of the state of the ones, and the sending forth is the act that the verse before names in the form of the statement of the evil of the deed, since the deed of the allying is the deed that the state of the ones does in the form of the thing that the next life takes as the deed of the one who allies with the disbelievers.

**The Closing of the State of the Transgression**

The verse closes with the statement of the state of the many of the ones in the form of the transgression, and the transgression is the state that the many are in in the form of the passing of the limits of the religion in the act of the allying with the disbelievers. The statement of the state of the many is the statement that the sūrah keeps for the close of the verse in the form of the naming of the state of the majority of the community of the book, and the naming is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the statement of the state that the many are in, since the state of the many is the state of the transgression that the act of the allying produces in the form of the passing of the limits of the religion. The sūrah keeps the verse in the form of the close of the account of the alliance, and the keeping is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the putting of the statement of the state of the many after the counterfactual of the belief and the naming of the choice, since the three together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the account of the state of the community of the book in the matter of the allying with the disbelievers.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:82
> You will surely find the most bitter towards the believers to be the Jews and polytheists and the most gracious to be those who call themselves Christian. That is because there are priests and monks among them and because they are not arrogant.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Two Attitudes the Verse Names**

The verse states the two attitudes of the two communities of the book toward the believers of the community of the sūrah in the form of the contrast of the most intense in the enmity and the nearest in the affection. The community of the law is named with the idolaters in the form of the most intense in the enmity to the believers, and the enmity is the state that the community is in in the form of the hostility to the believers of the community of the sūrah, and the state of the most intense is the state that the sūrah of the family names for the two communities in the verse of the clinging to the life more eagerly than any of the people, since the clinging to the life is the state that the two communities take in the form that the sūrah of the family keeps for the charge of the two. The community of the gospel is named in the form of the nearest in the affection to the believers, and the nearness is the state that the community is in in the form of the affection to the believers of the community of the sūrah, and the two states together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the contrast of the attitudes of the two communities.

**The Two Effects of the Two States**

Some of the commentators take the two states in the form of the two effects on the matter of the accepting of the truth of the Book and the mission of the messenger. The effect of the state of the enmity of the community of the law is the effect of the making difficult of the accepting of the truth by the community of the law, and the making difficult is the act that the state of the enmity does in the form of the state of the heart of the one who is in the enmity. The effect of the state of the nearness of the community of the gospel is the effect of the making easier of the accepting of the message of the Book by the community of the gospel, and the making easier is the act that the state of the nearness does in the form of the state of the heart of the one who is in the nearness, and the two effects together are the form that the reading keeps for the relation of the two states and the matter of the accepting of the message.

**The Measure of the Statement in the Readings**

Some of the commentators take the two statements of the verse as the statements that are the absolute in the form that is not limited to the certain situation of the history, and the taking is the act that the commentator does in the form of the reading of the statements as the general statements of the two communities. The reading is not the reading that the history supports, since at the several points of the history of the religion the community of the sūrah had the better relations with the community of the law than with the community of the gospel and the dynasties of the community of the gospel, and the two examples that the account names are the wars of the crossing of the sea and the war of the taking back of the land, and the two are the acts that the history keeps in the form of the evidence against the reading of the absolute. The reading that is the more plausible is the reading that puts the verse in the situation of the life of the messenger and the early community of the sūrah, and the putting is the act that the account does in the form of the contextualizing of the statement, since the members of the community of the law of the city and the surrounding region were in the state of the continual challenge to the authority of the messenger as the prophet, and some of them were reported to be in the state of the joining with the idolaters of the city of the birth in the harming of the messenger and the community, and the joining is the act that the account names as the form of the state of the enmity.

**The Figures of the Community of the Gospel**

The certain figures of the community of the gospel played the positive and the pivotal roles in the early life and the mission of the messenger, and the roles are the acts that the figures did in the form of the help of the messenger and the community. The monk who was the one of the desert recognized the messenger as the prophet in the early time of the life of the messenger, and the recognizing is the act that the figure did in the form of the seeing of the state of the messenger. The cousin of the wife of the messenger who was of the religion of the gospel identified the early revelations that the messenger received as the things that are the divine in the origin and the things that are consistent with the books of the gospel that he knew, and the identifying is the act that the figure did in the form of the naming of the state of the revelations. The king of the people of the land of the exile who was of the religion of the gospel sheltered the group of the believers of the city of the birth from the persecution, and the sheltering is the act that the king did in the form of the keeping of the believers as the guests in the land of the exile for the years until they could make the journey to the city of the refuge, and the king recognized the affinity between the teaching of the religion of the sūrah and his own belief in the form of the Christian, and the recognizing is the act that the king did in the form of the refusing of the handing over of the refugees to the tribe of the city of the birth who demanded the return of the refugees.

**The Two Marks of the Nearness**

The verse attributes the nearness of the community of the gospel to the believers in the two marks, and the two marks are the mark of the presence of the scholars of the religion and the devotees among the community, and the mark of the not being of the arrogance in the community. The word of the scholars of the religion is the word that some of the commentators take in the sense of the religious scholars of the community of the gospel, and the report of the companion who was formerly of the religion of the fire and then briefly of the religion of the gospel before the embracing of the religion of the sūrah carries the statement that the messenger explained the word in the sense of the truthful or the saintly ones of the community, and the explaining is the act that the report keeps in the form of the gloss of the word. The attitude of the religion of the sūrah toward the devotion of the seclusion of the community of the gospel is the attitude that is the mixed in the form of the several verses of the Book, since the institution of the seclusion has no parallel in the context of the religion of the sūrah and is in some of the ways inconsistent with the ideal of the religion of the balance of the obligations of the world and the devotions of the spirit, and the messenger discouraged the companions from the practice of the celibacy and the excessive self-denial. The sūrah of the emulation blames the certain devotees for the consuming of the wealth of the people in the false form and the turning from the way of God, and the sūrah of the emulation names the monasticism as the thing that the community of the gospel made up for itself and not the thing that God ordained for the community, and the sūrah of the family states that the community of the book is not all the same and that there is among the community the upright community that recites the signs of God in the watches of the night while they prostrate. The not being of the arrogance is the mark that the verse keeps for the nearness in the form of the second, and the arrogance is the vice that the Book connects in the several verses to the blindness of the spirit that leads the people to the rejection of the signs of God, and the signs include the prophets and the messages of the prophets, and the arrogance is contrasted in the Book with the attitude of the lowliness and the gratitude that moves the one to the prostration before God and the worship of God, and the two together are the form that the verse keeps for the mark of the not being of the arrogance in the community of the gospel, which is the mark of the inclination to the worship of God and the receptivity to the signs of God and the messages of the prophets.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:83
> When they listen to what has been revealed to the Messenger, you see their eyes overflowing with tears for recognizing the truth. They say, “Our Lord! We believe, so count us among the witnesses.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Hearing and the Overflowing of the Eyes**

The verse states the act of the community of the gospel in the form of the hearing of the thing that was sent down to the messenger, and the hearing is the act that the community does in the form of the receiving of the recitation of the Book. The verse states the state of the eyes of the community in the form of the overflowing with the tears, and the overflowing is the act that the eyes do in the form of the state of the recognition of the truth by the community, since the recognition of the truth is the act that the community does in the form of the seeing of the state of the revelation as the truth, and the seeing is the act that the state of the eyes takes in the form of the overflowing of the tears. The sūrah keeps the verse in the form of the account of the response of the community of the gospel to the Book, and the keeping is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the telling of the state of the community in the matter of the hearing of the revelation.

**The Occasion the Commentators Keep**

One of the early commentators claims that the verse came in the response to the first encounter of the believers with the king of the people of the land of the exile when the believers were seeking the shelter from the idolaters of the city of the birth, and the claim is the act that the commentator does in the form of the reading of the occasion. The claim is not the claim that the order of the time supports, since the sūrah is widely considered to have taken the revealing in the city of the refuge in its whole, and the original encounter with the king was in the time of the city of the birth, and the not supporting is the act that the account of the time does in the form of the objection to the claim. The most of the commentators take the occasion of the revealing as the later visit of the delegation that the king sent to the messenger in the city of the refuge, and the visit is the act that the account keeps in the form of the statement of the occasion. When the Book was recited to the delegation of the community of the gospel, the delegation began to weep and became the believers, and the weeping and the believing are the acts that the four commentators name as the form of the state of the delegation. Some of the accounts state that the delegation returned to the king and recited the Book to the king, and the king became the believer as well, and the returning and the reciting and the believing are the acts that the two accounts keep in the form of the statement, although there is no evidence of the history that the king later became of the religion of the sūrah, and the verse attributes the positive marks to the community of the gospel in the general form and not only to the ones who later embraced the religion of the sūrah, since the community of the gospel is described in the verse before as the nearest in the affection to the believers and not as the believers of the religion of the sūrah, and the sūrah of the criterion states the similar praise of the marks of the community of the gospel in the verse of the instilling of the compassion and the mercy into the hearts of the followers of the prophet of the gospel.

**The Belief That the Community States**

The community of the gospel states the belief in the form of the statement of the we in the belief in the messenger of the sūrah as the prophet that God sent and in the Book of the sūrah as the truth from God, and the statement is the act that the one commentator names as the form of the belief of the community. Some of the commentators take the response of the community as the reference to the Christians who heard the message that the messenger brought and believed in the prophethood of the messenger, although they continued to live in the form of the law of the prophet of the gospel or the way of the book of the gospel, and the continuing is the act that the community does in the form of the remaining of the community in the sense of the confession of the community of the gospel, since the remaining in the confession of the community is the act that the reading names in the form of the state of the community that believes in the messenger and lives in the law of the prophet of the gospel.

**The Plea of the Witnessing**

The community states the plea in the form of the recording of the community among the witnesses, and the plea is the act that the community does in the form of the asking of the state of the witnessing from God. The plea is taken by the three commentators in the two senses, and the two senses are the sense of the counting of the community among the ones who recognize the truth and the oneness of God, and the sense of the counting of the community among the religious community of the messenger specifically, and the two senses together are the form that the readings keep for the plea of the witnessing, since the witnessing is the state that the community asks for in the form of the thing that the community is to be counted among in the next life or in the form of the community of the religion of the sūrah.

**The Similar Responses in the Book**

The Book keeps the similar responses of the certain ones of the community of the book to the Book in the several verses, and the responses are the acts that the Book does in the form of the telling of the state of the ones who have the knowledge before the Book in the matter of the hearing of the recitation of the Book. The verse of the sūrah of the story of the night states that the ones who were given the knowledge before the Book fall upon their faces in the prostration when the Book is recited to them and say the glorification of the Lord and the statement of the fulfilling of the promise of the Lord, and they fall upon their faces weeping and are increased in the lowliness, and the stating is the act that the sūrah of the story does in the form of the parallel to the account of this verse, since the parallel of the two accounts is the act that the Book does in the form of the telling of the state of the ones of the knowledge in the matter of the hearing of the Book, and the sūrah of the story of the prophet who was sent to the people of the city keeps the similar account in the verses of the belief of the faithful of the scripture in the Book. The one of the commentators on this verse mentions the report that carries the command of the reciting of the Book and the weeping, and the command is the act that the report does in the form of the saying that the one is to recite the Book and weep, and if the weeping does not come in the natural form, the one is to put the weeping in the form of the act, and the report is the act that the commentator keeps in the form of the support of the state of the weeping in the response of the one of the knowledge to the Book.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:84
> Why should we not believe in Allah and the truth that has come to us? And we long for our Lord to include us in the company of the righteous.”

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Question of the Community**

The verse states the question of the community of the gospel in the form of the rhetorical question, and the question is the question of the not believing in God and the truth that came to the community. The question of the not believing is the form that the community uses for the statement of the belief, since the question that names the not believing is the question that the community does in the form of the answer to the state of the belief of the community, and the answer is the act that the community does in the form of the naming of the state of the belief as the state that the not believing does not fit the community. The truth that came to the community is the truth of the revelation of God in the form of the Book that was sent down to the messenger, and the coming of the truth is the act that the revelation does in the form of the putting of the truth in the state of the arrival to the community, since the truth that the community names in the question is the truth of the Book of the sūrah in the form that the community takes in the state of the hearing of the recitation.

**The Belief in the Oneness and the Truth**

The question of the community names the belief in God in the form of the belief in the oneness of God, and the oneness is the act that the belief of the community does in the form of the naming of the state of God that the community takes in the belief. The one commentator takes the belief of the community in the form of the belief in the oneness of God and the truth that came to the community through the revelations of God, and the taking is the act that the commentator does in the form of the reading of the two objects of the belief of the community. The response of the community as the whole is the response that some of the commentators take as the indication of the new awareness of the community of the need to leave the doctrines of the three in the nature of the prophet, and the indication is the act that the commentator does in the form of the reading of the response, since the response of the belief in the oneness of God and the truth of the Book is the response that the awareness of the leaving of the doctrines of the three takes in the form of the statement of the belief of the community.

**The Desire of the Company of the Righteous**

The verse states the desire of the community in the form of the asking of the Lord to admit the community among the righteous, and the desire is the act that the community does in the form of the asking of the state of the company of the righteous. The righteous that the community names are the ones that the commentators take in the two readings, and the two readings are the reading of the messenger and the companions of the messenger, and the reading of the ones who affirm the oneness of God in the general form, and the two readings together are the form that the commentators keep for the object of the desire of the community. The desire of the community of the company of the righteous is the desire that some of the commentators take as the sign of the belief of the community, since the disbelievers do not desire the thing of the company of the righteous, and the not desiring is the act that the state of the disbelief does in the form of the state of the heart of the one who is in the disbelief, and the desiring is the act that the state of the belief does in the form of the state of the heart of the one who is in the belief.

**The Two Objects of the Question**

The question of the verse keeps the two objects of the belief of the community in the form of the God and the truth, and the two objects are the objects that the question names in the form of the statement of the belief. The first of the objects is God in the form of the oneness of God, and the second of the objects is the truth in the form of the thing that came to the community through the revelation of God, and the two together are the form that the question keeps for the state of the belief of the community, since the belief of the community is the belief that the question states in the form of the two objects held together in the one question. The sūrah keeps the question in the verse of the response of the community, and the keeping is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the telling of the state of the belief of the community in the matter of the two objects of the belief, which is the telling of the state of the community that believes in God and the truth and desires the company of the righteous.

**The Verse in the Account of the Delegation**

The verse stands in the sūrah in the place where it completes the account of the response of the community of the gospel to the Book, and the completing is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the placing of the question of the community after the statement of the belief and the plea of the witnessing in the verse before. The verse before is the verse that states the hearing of the community and the overflowing of the eyes and the statement of the belief and the plea of the recording among the witnesses, and this verse is the verse that states the question of the not believing and the desire of the company of the righteous, and the two together are the account that the sūrah keeps for the response of the community of the gospel to the Book, which is the account of the hearing and the weeping and the belief and the plea and the question and the desire held together in the two verses, and the six together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the state of the community of the gospel in the matter of the receiving of the Book.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:85
> So Allah will reward them for what they said with Gardens under which rivers flow, to stay there forever. And that is the reward of the good-doers.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Reward That the Verse Names**

The verse states the act of God for the community of the gospel in the form of the rewarding of the community for the thing that the community said, and the rewarding is the act that God does in the form of the giving of the gardens to the community. The thing that the community said is the thing that the two verses before state in the form of the statement of the belief and the plea of the witnessing and the question of the not believing and the desire of the company of the righteous, and the saying is the act that the community does in the form of the words of the response of the community to the Book. The sūrah keeps the verse in the form of the close of the account of the response of the community, and the keeping is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the putting of the reward after the saying of the community, since the putting of the reward after the saying is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the statement of the relation of the saying of the community and the act of God that the saying produces.

**The Gardens With the Rivers Beneath**

The verse describes the reward in the form of the gardens with the rivers running beneath them, and the description is the description that the Book keeps for the reward of the believers and the virtuous in the several verses of the other sūrahs. The image of the gardens with the rivers beneath is the image that the sūrah of the family states for the good news of the ones who believe and do the good in the verse of the reward of the next life, and the stating is the act that the sūrah of the family does in the form of the naming of the state of the reward, and the naming of the state of the reward is the act that the Book does in the form of the repetition of the image in the verses that name the reward of the believers and the virtuous in the next life. The verse of this sūrah keeps the image in the form of the reward of the community of the gospel, and the keeping is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the putting of the image in the account of the response of the community, since the account of the response of the community is the account that the sūrah keeps for the state of the community that believes in the messenger and the Book and desires the company of the righteous, and the state of the community is the state that the image of the gardens names in the form of the reward.

**The Residence in the Eternity**

The verse states the state of the community in the gardens in the form of the residing in the eternity, and the residing is the act that the community does in the form of the being in the gardens in the state that is not the state of the leaving. The eternity of the residing is the state that the verse names for the community in the form of the statement of the permanence of the reward, and the permanence is the act that the verse does in the form of the putting of the reward in the form that is not the form of the thing that ends, since the reward of the next life is the reward that the sūrah keeps in the form of the thing that does not end in the state of the community that is the resident of the gardens, and the not ending is the act that the verse does in the form of the naming of the state of the gardens in the next life.

**The Recompense of the Good-Doers**

The verse closes with the statement of the reward in the form of the recompense of the good-doers, and the good-doers are the ones that the verse names in the form of the state of the community of the gospel in the matter of the saying and the belief and the desire of the company of the righteous. The statement of the recompense of the good-doers is the statement that the sūrah keeps for the close of the verse in the form of the naming of the state of the one who is the recompensed, and the naming is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the putting of the community in the state of the good-doer, since the state of the community of the gospel is the state of the good-doer in the form that the verse names, and the good-deed of the community is the deed of the belief and the saying and the desire of the company of the righteous that the two verses before state in the form of the account of the response of the community to the Book.

**The Verse in the Account of the Community of the Gospel**

The sūrah keeps this verse in the place where it completes the account of the community of the gospel in the form of the reward, and the completing is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the placing of the verse of the reward at the end of the account of the response of the community. The account of the community of the gospel runs from the verse of the nearness in the affection to the verse of the reward of the gardens, and the running is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the telling of the state of the community in the several marks, which are the mark of the nearness in the affection, the mark of the hearing and the weeping, the mark of the belief and the plea, the mark of the question and the desire, and the mark of the reward, and the five marks together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the account of the community of the gospel in the middle of the sūrah, and the account is the account that stands in the place where the sūrah turns from the charge of the community of the book to the command of the community of the sūrah in the matter of the lawful and the unlawful.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:86
> As for those who disbelieve and reject Our signs, they will be the residents of the Hellfire.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The State That the Verse Names**

The verse states the state of the ones in the form of the disbelief and the denial of the signs, and the two marks are the marks that the verse keeps for the state of the one who is not the one of the belief. The disbelief is the state of the one who does not take the message of the Book in the form of the belief, and the denial of the signs is the act of the one who puts the signs of God in the form of the thing that the one does not take as the truth, and the two together are the form that the verse keeps for the state of the one who is the resident of the fire, since the state of the one who disbelieves and denies the signs is the state that the verse names in the form of the thing that is the opposite of the state of the one who believes and does the good.

**The Abode That the Verse Keeps**

The verse states the abode of the ones in the form of the fire, and the fire is the abode that the Book keeps for the disbelievers and the ones who deny the signs of God in the next life in the several verses. The verse of the sūrah of the family states the same statement in the form of the residents of the fire who abide in it forever, and the stating is the act that the sūrah of the family does in the form of the parallel of the statement, and the parallel of the statement is the act that the Book does in the form of the repetition of the naming of the abode of the disbelievers in the verses of the other sūrahs that name the fire as the abode of the ones who disbelieve and deny the signs, and the naming of the abode is the act that the Book does in the form of the statement of the state of the next life of the one who is not the one of the belief.

**The Contrast With the Verse Before**

The sūrah keeps this verse and the verse before in the form of the contrast of the two states, and the contrast is the contrast of the reward of the gardens and the abode of the fire. The verse before states the reward of the community of the gospel in the form of the gardens with the rivers beneath, and this verse states the abode of the ones who disbelieve and deny the signs in the form of the fire, and the two together are the structure that the sūrah gives to the matter of the two states of the community in the next life, which is the structure of the reward and the abode held together in the two verses. The sūrah does the contrast in the form of the putting of the verse of the abode after the verse of the reward, and the putting is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the statement of the two destinies of the community, since the two destinies are the destinies that the sūrah keeps for the state of the one in the matter of the belief and the disbelief, and the statement of the two destinies is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the close of the account of the community of the book and the opening of the account of the command of the community of the sūrah.

**The Signs That Are Denied**

The verse names the signs in the form of the thing that the ones deny, and the signs are the signs of God that the Book states in the form of the revelation and the account and the command. The denying of the signs is the act of the one who puts the signs in the form of the thing that the one does not take as the truth, and the not taking is the act that the state of the disbelief does in the form of the state of the heart of the one who is in the disbelief. The sūrah keeps the verse in the form of the charge of the denying of the signs, and the keeping is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the putting of the charge at the end of the account of the community of the book, since the account of the community of the book is the account that the sūrah tells from the verse of the charge of the vengeance to the verse of this charge, and the charge of the denying of the signs is the charge that the sūrah does in the form of the naming of the state of the one who does not take the signs of God in the form of the truth, and the naming of the state is the act that the verse does in the form of the statement of the abode of the one who is in the state.

**The Turning of the Sūrah at the Verse**

The verse stands in the sūrah in the place where the sūrah turns from the account of the community of the book to the command of the community of the sūrah in the matter of the lawful and the unlawful, and the turning is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the placing of the verse of the charge at the end of the section of the account of the community of the book. The section of the account runs from the verse of the question of the vengeance to the verse of this charge, and the running is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the telling of the state of the community of the book in the matter of the belief and the disbelief and the doctrine and the alliance and the attitude toward the community of the sūrah, and the telling of the section is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the account of the community of the book, since the account of the community of the book is the account that the sūrah keeps in the middle of the sūrah, and the account ends in the verse of this charge, and the charge is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the close of the account of the community of the book in the matter of the denial of the signs of God.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:87
> O believers! Do not forbid the good things which Allah has made lawful for you, and do not transgress. Indeed, Allah does not like transgressors.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Prohibition of the Forbidding**

The verse states the command of the community of the sūrah in the form of the not making of the forbidden to themselves of the thing that God made lawful to them, and the not making is the act that the command forbids in the form of the charge of the state of the one who puts the lawful thing in the form of the forbidden. The command is the command that the sūrah does in the form of the address of the community, and the addressing is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the putting of the command in the account of the matter of the lawful and the unlawful, since the account of the matter of the lawful and the unlawful is the account that the sūrah opens in this verse after the account of the community of the book, and the opening of the account is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the turning from the charge of the denial of the signs to the command of the not forbidding of the lawful.

**The Measure of the Lawful in the Book**

The Book makes the distinction between the lawful and the unlawful in the matter of the food in the several verses, and the distinguishing is the act that the Book does in the form of the naming of the things that are the forbidden and the things that are the lawful. The verses that name the forbidden things are the verses that state the forbidding of the carrion and the blood and the swine and the thing that is slaughtered in the name of the other than God, and the stating is the act that the sūrah of the family and the sūrah of the table spread and the sūrah of the light and this sūrah do in the form of the repetition of the naming of the four forbidden things, and the naming of the four things is the act that the Book does in the form of the statement of the measure of the forbidden, since the measure of the forbidden is the measure that the Book keeps for the things that are the forbidden in the matter of the food, and the measure is the measure that is the narrow in the form that the verses of the Book keep for the forbidden things, and the narrowness of the measure of the forbidden is the act that the verses do in the form of the statement of the wide range of the lawful foods that the community of the sūrah has in the state of the eating and the enjoying freely.

**The Criticism of the Arbitrary Forbidding**

The Book takes in the criticism the ones who forbid the lawful things in the arbitrary form without the warrant of God, and the criticism is the act that the Book does in the form of the charge of the state of the one who makes the unlawful of the lawful thing in the form of the word of the tongue without the authority of the Book. The verse of the sūrah of the light that states the not declaring of the false with the tongues that this is the lawful and that is the unlawful in the form of the fabricating of the lie against God is the verse that the Book keeps for the charge of the arbitrary forbidding, and the stating is the act that the sūrah of the light does in the form of the parallel to the command of this verse, since the parallel of the charge and the command is the act that the Book does in the form of the statement of the state of the one who forbids the lawful thing without the warrant of God. The verse of the sūrah of the story of the one who claimed the lordship that asks who forbids the adornments and the lawful provisions that God brought forth for His servants is the verse that the Book keeps for the charge of the forbidding of the lawful provisions in the form of the question, and the question is the act that the sūrah of the story does in the form of the putting of the adornments and the lawful provisions in the state of the thing that God brought forth for the servants, and the two verses together are the form that the Book keeps for the criticism of the arbitrary forbidding of the lawful things.

**The Transgression That the Verse Forbids**

The verse states the command of the not exceeding of the limits in the form of the second command after the command of the not forbidding, and the exceeding is the act that the command forbids in the form of the passing of the limits of the religion in the matter of the eating and the enjoying. The limits that the verse names are the limits that the Book sets in the matter of the lawful things, and the setting of the limits is the act that the Book does in the form of the naming of the measure of the eating and the enjoying, since the measure of the eating is the measure that the Book keeps for the community of the sūrah in the form of the eating of the lawful and the good without the passing of the limit, and the passing of the limit is the act that the verse forbids in the form of the command of the not exceeding. The sūrah keeps the two commands in the verse in the form of the not forbidding of the lawful and the not exceeding of the limits, and the two together are the structure that the sūrah gives to the command of the community in the matter of the lawful things, which is the structure of the keeping of the lawful in the state of the lawful and the keeping of the limit in the state of the not passing.

**The Closing of the Verse**

The verse closes with the statement that God does not like the transgressors, and the statement is the statement that the sūrah keeps for the close of the command in the form of the naming of the state of God for the state of the one who exceeds the limits. The statement of the not liking of the transgressors is the statement that the Book states in the several verses in the same form, and the stating is the act that the sūrah of the family and the sūrah of the story of the one who claimed the lordship do in the form of the repetition of the statement, since the statement of the not liking of the transgressors is the statement that the Book keeps for the state of the one who passes the limits of God in the form of the naming of the state of God for the transgressor, and the sūrah of the story of the judgment states the sealing of the hearts of the transgressors in the verse of the messengers who came with the clear proofs, and the sealing is the act that the sūrah of the story does in the form of the statement of the consequence of the state of the transgression.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:88
> Eat of the good, lawful things provided to you by Allah. And be mindful of Allah in Whom you believe.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Command of the Eating**

The verse states the command of the eating of the thing that God provided for the community, and the thing is the thing that is the lawful and the good in the form that the verse names. The command is the command that the sūrah does in the form of the address of the community, and the addressing is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the putting of the command after the command of the verse before, since the command of the verse before is the command of the not forbidding of the lawful and the not exceeding of the limits, and the command of this verse is the command of the eating of the lawful and the good, and the two commands together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the account of the matter of the lawful things, which is the form of the command of the not forbidding and the command of the eating held together in the two verses.

**The Provision of God in the Form of the Lawful and the Good**

The verse describes the thing that the community is to eat in the form of the provision of God that is the lawful and the good, and the describing is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the naming of the two marks of the provision. The mark of the lawful is the mark that the provision takes in the form of the not being of the forbidden in the matter of the eating, and the mark of the good is the mark that the provision takes in the form of the being of the quality of the goodness in the thing that the community eats, and the two marks together are the form that the verse keeps for the provision of God in the matter of the food. The sūrah of the family states the similar command in the verse of the address to the humanity to eat from what is the lawful and the good on the earth and not to follow the footsteps of Satan, and the stating is the act that the sūrah of the family does in the form of the parallel of the command, and the sūrah of the family states the similar command in the verse of the address to the believers to eat from the good things that God provided and to give thanks to God, and the sūrah of the light states the similar command in the verse of the eating of the good and the lawful things that God provided, and the three verses together are the form that the Book keeps for the command of the eating of the provision of God in the state of the lawful and the good.

**The Reverting to God in the Belief**

The verse closes with the command of the revering of God in the form of the address of the community as the ones who believe, and the revering is the act that the command puts in the form of the duty of the community in the matter of the eating. The revering of God is the act that the community does in the form of the putting of the state of the fear and the lowliness before God in the matter of the eating of the provision of God, and the putting of the state is the act that the command does in the form of the statement of the relation of the eating and the state of the community before God, since the eating of the provision of God is the act that the community does in the state of the belief, and the state of the belief is the state that the verse names in the form of the address of the community as the ones who believe in God, and the revering of God is the act that the state of the belief carries in the form of the duty of the community in the matter of the eating.

**The Verse in the Account of the Lawful**

The sūrah keeps this verse in the place where it completes the opening of the account of the matter of the lawful things, and the completing is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the placing of the command of the eating after the command of the not forbidding. The account of the matter of the lawful things is the account that the sūrah opens in the two verses, and the two verses are the verses of the command of the not forbidding of the lawful and the command of the eating of the lawful and the good, and the two together are the opening of the account that the sūrah keeps for the matter of the lawful things in the middle of the sūrah, since the account of the sūrah runs from the account of the community of the book to the account of the matter of the lawful things and then to the account of the oaths and the account of the four things that are the defilement, and the running of the account is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the telling of the state of the community of the sūrah in the matter of the eating and the oaths and the avoidance of the defilement.

**The Two Verses in the Structure**

The sūrah keeps the two verses of the account of the lawful things in the form of the structure of the prohibition and the command, and the structure is the structure of the not doing of the act of the forbidding and the doing of the act of the eating. The verse of the not forbidding is the verse that states the charge of the state of the one who makes the forbidden of the lawful thing, and the verse of the eating is the verse that states the command of the act of the eating of the lawful and the good, and the two together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the matter of the lawful things in the state of the community of the sūrah, since the matter of the lawful things is the matter that the sūrah does in the form of the keeping of the community in the state of the eating of the provision of God without the forbidding of the lawful and without the exceeding of the limits, and the keeping of the community in the state is the act that the two verses do in the form of the opening of the account of the matter of the lawful things in the middle of the sūrah.
