## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:89
> Allah will not call you to account for your thoughtless oaths, but He will hold you accountable for deliberate oaths. The penalty for a broken oath is to feed ten poor people from what you normally feed your own family, or to clothe them, or to free a bondsperson. But if none of this is affordable, then you must fast three days. This is the penalty for breaking your oaths. So be mindful of your oaths. This is how Allah makes things clear to you, so perhaps you will be grateful.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Two Kinds of the Oath**

The verse states the two kinds of the oath in the form of the careless oath and the earnest oath, and the two kinds are the kinds that the verse keeps for the oath of the one in the matter of the accountability of God. The careless oath is the oath that the one takes in the form of the word that does not carry the meaning of the oath in the state of the tongue of the one, and the earnest oath is the oath that the one pledges in the form of the word that carries the meaning of the oath in the state of the heart of the one, and the two together are the form that the verse keeps for the oath in the matter of the accountability. The Book states the similar distinction in the verse of the sūrah of the family that names the oath that is not the intentional in the form of the oath for which God does not hold the one accountable, and the stating is the act that the sūrah of the family does in the form of the parallel of the distinction, since the parallel of the two distinctions is the act that the Book does in the form of the statement of the state of the oath in the matter of the accountability of God.

**The Oath Against the Lawful Thing**

The careless oath of the verse is, in the most specific reference, the oath that the one takes in the arbitrary form to avoid the certain lawful foods, and the taking is the act that the one does in the form of the following of the command of the two verses before, which are the verses of the not forbidding of the lawful and the eating of the lawful and the good. The oath against the lawful thing does not make the lawful thing the forbidden in the form of the actual forbidding of God, and the not making is the act that the reading of one of the commentators states in the form of the meaning of the oath, since the meaning of the oath against the lawful thing is the meaning that the reading keeps in the form of the statement that the oath does not change the state of the thing in the law of God, and the not changing is the act that the reading does in the form of the statement of the state of the lawful thing after the oath against it. The Book keeps the warning against the misuse of the oaths in the several verses, and the warning is the act that the Book does in the form of the charge of the state of the one who uses the oaths in the form of the excuse of the not doing of the good or the not guarding against the evil, and the charge of the trading of the covenant of God and the oaths for the fleeting gain is the charge that the sūrah of the family keeps in the form of the statement of the state of the one who trades the oaths, and the Book criticizes the disbelievers and the hypocrites for the breaking of the oaths or the using of them in the deceptive form in the several verses of the other sūrahs.

**The Expiation in the Three Forms**

The verse states the expiation of the oath in the three forms, and the three forms are the form of the feeding of the ten poor people, the form of the clothing of the poor people, and the form of the freeing of the slave. The feeding of the ten poor people is understood by the commentators in the several ways, and the ways are the way of the providing of the combination of the bread and the dates and the oil, and the way of the provision of the meat as the best of the provision, and the way of the certain amount of the wheat or the other grain, and the way of the feeding of the lunch and the supper, and the several ways together are the form that the commentators keep for the measure of the feeding. The clothing of the poor people may include the giving of the one or the two articles of the clothing, and the article sometimes includes the covering of the head in the specific form, and the giving is the act that the expiation does in the form of the provision of the clothing to the poor. The freeing of the slave is the act that the expiation does in the form of the setting free of the slave who is in the good health without the physical deficiency. For both of the forms of the feeding and the clothing, some of the commentators state that the recipient must be the truly poor and cannot be the dependent or the close family member for whom the one is the responsible in any case, and some indicate that the recipient must be the free person, and there is some disagreement among the commentators about whether the recipient must be of the religion of the sūrah or not, and the school of the law of the first of the companions of the messenger allows the food or the clothing to be given to the poor among the other religious communities, and the verse of the sūrah of the criterion that states that God does not forbid the dealing kindly and fairly with the ones who have not fought the community is the verse that the Book keeps for the matter of the giving of the charity to the non-Muslim in the form of the statement of the permission.

**The Fasting for the One Who Does Not Find**

The verse states the form of the fasting for the one who does not find the means of the expiation in the feeding or the clothing or the freeing, and the fasting is the act that the one does in the form of the three days. Some of the commentators take the fasting as the fasting of the three consecutive days, and the taking is the act that the commentators do in the form of the reading of the consecutive form of the fasting. The other of the commentators hold that the consecutive form is not the required, since the verse does not state the consecutive form in the specific way as the verse of the sūrah of the pen states it for the certain kind of the oath against the wife, which is the oath that mandates the fasting of the two consecutive months, and the not stating is the act that the reading does in the form of the objection to the reading of the consecutive form. The fasting is the sufficient expiation only for the one who does not have the means of the providing for the poor, and the sufficiency is the act that the reading of one of the commentators states in the form of the limiting of the fasting to the one who does not find the means, since the fasting is the form of the expiation that the verse keeps for the one who is not able to do the expiation in the feeding or the clothing or the freeing.

**The Closing of the Command of the Keeping**

The verse closes with the command of the keeping of the oaths in the form of the statement that the oaths are to be kept in the state of the truth, and the keeping is the act that the command puts in the form of the duty of the one who swears the oath. The command of the keeping is the command that follows the statement of the expiation, and the following is the act that the verse does in the form of the putting of the command after the expiation, since the putting of the command of the keeping after the statement of the expiation is the act that the verse does in the form of the statement of the state of the oath in the matter of the truth of the one who swears, and the sūrah keeps the closing of the verse in the form of the statement that God makes the signs clear to the community in order that the community may be grateful, and the making clear is the act that God does in the form of the putting of the signs in the state of the clearness for the community, and the gratitude is the act that the community is to do in the form of the response to the making clear of the signs, and the two together are the form that the verse keeps for the close of the account of the oaths.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:90
> O believers! Intoxicants, gambling, idols, and drawing lots for decisions are all evil of Satan’s handiwork. So shun them so you may be successful.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Four Things That the Verse Names**

The verse names the four things in the form of the charge of the defilement, and the four things are the intoxicants and the gambling and the stone altars and the divining arrows. The word of the intoxicants is the word that technically refers to the drink made from the grapes, and the word is the word that the reading of the second of the caliphs explains in the sermon in the form of the statement that the intoxicant is the thing that covers the intellect, and the explaining is the act that the sermon does in the form of the extension of the prohibition to all of the things that cloud the mind when they are consumed, since the extension of the prohibition to all of the intoxicants is the act that the reading does in the form of the statement of the meaning of the word. The word of the gambling is the word that refers to the specific form of the gambling that was popular in the time before the religion of the sūrah, and the word is the word that the sūrah of the family names in the verse of the question of the intoxicants and the gambling, and the verse is understood as the application to all of the forms of the gambling in the same way that the prohibition of the intoxicants is understood as the prohibition of all of the intoxicants. The word of the stone altars is likely the reference to the idolatrous practice of the sacrificing of the animals on the stone altars, and the practice is the practice that this sūrah names in the verse of the forbidden things as the practice that makes the meat the unlawful to consume. The word of the divining arrows is the reference to the pagan practice of the drawing of the lots with the arrows for the decisions of the food and the other goods, and the practice is the practice that the verse of the forbidden things of this sūrah names in the form of the forbidding of the drawing of the lots for the decisions.

**The Charge of the Defilement**

The verse states the four things in the form of the charge of the defilement, and the defilement is the state that the four things carry in the form of the thing that brings the spiritual impurity to the one. The charge of the defilement is the charge that the sūrah does in the form of the naming of the state of the four things, and the naming of the state is the act that the verse does in the form of the putting of the four things in the state of the thing that is not the thing of the purity of the spirit, since the state of the four things is the state of the defilement that the verse charges in the form of the statement of the spiritual impurity of the four things, and the spiritual impurity is the state that the verse puts in the form of the charge against the taking of the four things in the act of the community.

**The Work of the One Who Leads Astray**

The verse attributes the four things to the work of the one who leads astray, and the attributing is the act that the verse does in the form of the naming of the agent of the four things. The work of the one who leads astray is the work that the verse names in the form of the thing that the four things are in the state of the production of the one who leads astray, and the production is the act that the verse does in the form of the statement of the origin of the four things, since the origin of the four things is the origin of the one who leads astray in the form that the verse names, and the naming of the origin is the act that the verse does in the form of the putting of the four things in the work of the one who leads astray, and the putting of the four things in the work of the one who leads astray is the act that the verse does in the form of the charge of the four things in the matter of the spiritual impurity.

**The Command of the Avoiding**

The verse states the command of the avoiding of the four things in the form of the putting of the four things in the state of the thing that the community is not to take, and the avoiding is the act that the command puts in the form of the duty of the community. The command of the avoiding is the command that the sūrah does in the form of the address of the community, and the avoiding of the four things is the avoiding that is the complete in the form of the not taking of the four things in any of the forms of the taking, since the prohibition of the intoxicants includes not only the consuming of the intoxicants but also the selling of the intoxicants and the profit that is attained by the selling, and the including is the act that the reading of the commentator states in the form of the extension of the prohibition. The command of the avoiding is the command that the sūrah does in the form of the closing of the charge of the defilement, and the closing is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the putting of the command after the charge, since the putting of the command of the avoiding after the charge of the defilement and the naming of the work of the one who leads astray is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the structure of the verse, which is the structure of the naming of the four things and the charge of the defilement and the naming of the work of the one who leads astray and the command of the avoiding held together in the one verse.

**The Verse in the Order of the Prohibition**

The verse stands in the sūrah in the place where it is the fourth and the final passage in the order of the time of the revealing that relates to the intoxicating drinks, and the standing is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the placing of the verse of the final prohibition at the point of the completing of the account of the matter of the intoxicants. The first of the passages is the passage of the sūrah of the light that presents the image of the fruits from which the community derives the strong drink in the form of the sign of the generous provision of God, and the second of the passages is the passage of the sūrah of the family that states that the intoxicants and the gambling have some of the benefit and great of the sin, and the third of the passages is the passage of the sūrah of the family that commands the community to avoid the prayer in the state of the drunkenness, and the fourth of the passages is the passage of this verse that states the final and the complete prohibition of the intoxicants, and the four passages together are the form that the Book keeps for the account of the matter of the intoxicants in the order of the time of the revealing, since the account of the matter of the intoxicants is the account that the Book does in the form of the movement from the presentation of the provision to the statement of the benefit and the sin and the command of the avoiding of the prayer in the drunkenness and the final prohibition of the intoxicants, and the four passages together are the structure that the Book gives to the matter of the intoxicants in the form of the gradual prohibition. The accounts report that this verse took the revealing after some of the companions of the messenger became the drunk and began the fighting with one another until one of the men was seriously injured, and the report is the act that the account keeps in the form of the statement of the occasion of the verse, since the occasion of the fighting of the drunk is the occasion that the account of the occasion does in the form of the support of the statement of the final prohibition of the intoxicants in the verse.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:91
> Satan’s plan is to stir up hostility and hatred between you with intoxicants and gambling and to prevent you from remembering Allah and praying. Will you not then abstain?

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Work of the One Who Leads Astray**

The verse states the work of the one who leads astray in the form of the sowing of the enmity and the hatred among the community, and the work is the work that the one who leads astray does in the form of the means of the intoxicants and the gambling. The sowing of the enmity and the hatred is the act that the one who leads astray does to the community in the form of the putting of the two states of the enmity and the hatred in the heart of the one who takes the intoxicants and the gambling, and the putting of the two states is the act that the verse charges in the form of the naming of the work of the one who leads astray. The sūrah keeps the verse in the form of the statement of the purpose of the prohibition of the four things in the verse before, and the keeping is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the putting of the statement of the purpose after the command of the avoiding, since the putting of the purpose after the command is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the structure of the two verses, which is the structure of the charge of the defilement and the command of the avoiding in the verse before and the statement of the work of the one who leads astray and the question of the refraining in this verse.

**The Two Means and the Two Turnings**

The verse names the two means of the work of the one who leads astray in the form of the intoxicants and the gambling, and the two means are the things that the one who leads astray uses in the form of the putting of the enmity and the hatred in the community. The verse names the two turnings that the two means produce in the form of the turning from the remembrance of God and the turning from the prayer, and the two turnings are the acts that the two means do to the community in the form of the taking of the community away from the two acts of the remembrance and the prayer. The two means and the two turnings together are the form that the verse keeps for the work of the one who leads astray, since the work of the one who leads astray is the work that the verse states in the form of the means and the turnings held together in the one statement, and the statement of the means and the turnings is the act that the verse does in the form of the naming of the state of the community that takes the two means in the form of the state of the enmity and the hatred and the turning from the remembrance and the prayer.

**The Enmity as the Fate of the Two Communities**

The sūrah keeps the statement of the enmity and the hatred among the community in the form of the fate of the two communities of the book in the two verses of the sūrah. The verse of the covenant of the community of the gospel states the letting arise of the hostility and the enmity between the members of the community of the gospel until the day of the judgment in the form of the result of the neglecting of a portion of what the community was commanded to uphold, and the letting arise is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the naming of the state of the community of the gospel in the matter of the enmity. The verse of the charge of the shackled hand states the sowing of the enmity and the hatred among the community of the law until the day of the resurrection in the form of the punishment of the defiance and the disbelief of the community of the law, and the sowing is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the naming of the state of the community of the law in the matter of the enmity. The two verses together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the state of the enmity and the hatred among the community, and this verse states the same state in the form of the work of the one who leads astray through the two means, since the state of the enmity and the hatred among the community is the state that the two verses of the two communities and this verse of the community of the sūrah keep in the form of the parallel of the fate of the enmity, and the parallel of the three verses is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the statement of the state of the enmity as the state that the community that does not take the remembrance and the prayer in the form of the act is in.

**The Question of the Refraining**

The verse closes with the question of the refraining, and the question is the form that the sūrah does in the act of the inviting of the community to the act of the avoiding of the four things. The question of the refraining is the question that the sūrah keeps in the form of the address of the community in the state of the command of the avoiding in the verse before, and the addressing is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the putting of the question after the statement of the work of the one who leads astray, since the putting of the question after the statement of the work is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the invitation of the community to the taking of the command of the avoiding in the form of the act, and the invitation of the community to the act of the refraining is the act that the question does in the form of the closing of the verse.

**The Two Verses in the Complete Prohibition**

The sūrah keeps the two verses of the account of the four things in the form of the complete prohibition of the intoxicants and the gambling and the stone altars and the divining arrows, and the complete prohibition is the act that the two verses do in the form of the charge of the defilement and the statement of the work of the one who leads astray and the command of the avoiding and the question of the refraining. The two verses are the two verses that complete the account of the matter of the intoxicants in the order of the time of the revealing, and the completing is the act that the two verses do in the form of the putting of the final prohibition at the end of the account of the four passages of the matter of the intoxicants. The accounts report that the two verses took the revealing after some of the companions of the messenger became the drunk and began the fighting with one another until one of the men was seriously injured, and the report is the act that the account keeps in the form of the statement of the occasion of the two verses, since the occasion of the fighting of the drunk is the occasion that the account does in the form of the support of the statement of the complete prohibition of the intoxicants and the gambling in the two verses, and the statement of the complete prohibition is the act that the two verses do in the form of the close of the account of the matter of the four things in the middle of the sūrah.

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:92
> Obey Allah and obey the Messenger and beware! But if you turn away, then know that Our Messenger’s duty is only to deliver ˹the message˺ clearly.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Command of the Two Obeyings**

The verse states the command of the obeying of God and the obeying of the messenger in the form of the two commands held together in the one verse, and the two commands are the commands that the Book states in the several verses of the other sūrahs. The verses that carry the two commands are the verse of the sūrah of the family that states the saying of the obeying of God and His messenger and the statement of the not liking of God for the disbelievers who turn away, and the verse of the sūrah of the family that commands the obeying of God and the messenger and the ones in authority among the community, and the verse of the sūrah of the light that states the obeying of God and the messenger and the statement of the duty of the messenger as the delivering of the message clearly, and the stating of the two commands is the act that the Book does in the form of the repetition of the command of the obeying in the sūrahs of the family and the light and the story of the night and the story of the letters and the story of the pen and the story of the gathering and the story of the belief and the story of the criterion. In this context of the sūrah of the table spread, the command is likely the command that encourages the obeying of the prohibitions that are listed in the two verses of the four things, and the encouraging is the act that the command does in the form of the putting of the community in the state of the obeying of the avoiding of the intoxicants and the gambling and the stone altars and the divining arrows.

**The Clear Proclamation as the Duty of the Messenger**

The verse states the duty of the messenger in the form of the delivering of the clear message, and the delivering is the act that the verse names as the only duty of the messenger in the matter of the message. This is one of the several places of the Book where the messenger is reminded that his only duty is the delivering of the clear proclamation, and the reminding is the act that the Book does in the form of the repetition of the statement of the duty in the verses of the other sūrahs that name the duty of the messenger as the delivering of the message, and the naming of the duty is the act that the Book does in the form of the statement of the state of the messenger in the matter of the message, since the state of the messenger is the state of the one whose duty is the delivering of the message and not the state of the one who compels the community to the taking of the message.

**The Not Being the Keeper of the Community**

The Book reminds the messenger in the several verses that he is not the keeper of the community, and the reminding is the act that the Book does in the form of the statement of the state of the messenger in the matter of the reaction of the community to the message. The verse of the sūrah of the family states that the one who obeys the messenger has truly obeyed God, and that the messenger was not sent as the keeper over the community, and the stating is the act that the sūrah of the family does in the form of the parallel of the statement of this verse, since the parallel of the two statements is the act that the Book does in the form of the repetition of the statement of the not being of the messenger as the keeper of the community. The verse of the sūrah of the story of the night states that God did not appoint the messenger as the keeper of the community and that the messenger is not the maintainer of the community, and the stating is the act that the sūrah of the story does in the form of the parallel of the statement, and the two verses together are the form that the Book keeps for the state of the messenger in the matter of the reaction of the community to the message, which is the form of the not being of the responsibility of the messenger for the ones who remain the unmoved by the message.

**The Wariness That the Verse Commands**

The verse states the command of the wariness in the form of the third command between the two commands of the obeying and the statement of the turning away, and the wariness is the act that the command puts in the form of the duty of the community in the matter of the obeying of the two. The wariness is the state that the community is to take in the form of the guarding of the community against the turning away from the obeying of God and the messenger, and the guarding is the act that the command does in the form of the putting of the community in the state of the attention to the state of the obedience, since the state of the wariness is the state that the community is to keep in the matter of the obeying of the two in the form of the act of the avoiding of the turning away. The sūrah keeps the three commands and the statement of the turning away in the verse in the form of the structure of the command and the consequence, and the structure is the structure of the obeying of the two and the wariness and the turning away and the statement of the duty of the messenger, and the four together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the verse of the close of the account of the prohibitions.

**The Verse in the Account of the Prohibitions**

The sūrah keeps this verse in the place where it closes the account of the four things and the oaths and the lawful and the unlawful, and the closing is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the placing of the command of the obeying at the end of the account of the prohibitions. The account of the prohibitions runs from the verse of the not forbidding of the lawful to the verse of this command of the obeying, and the running is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the telling of the state of the community in the matter of the eating and the oaths and the avoiding of the four things, and the telling of the account is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the command of the obeying of God and the messenger at the end of the account, since the command of the obeying is the command that the sūrah does in the form of the putting of the community in the state of the obeying of all of the commands of the account in the one act of the obeying, and the putting of the community in the state of the obeying is the act that the verse does in the form of the close of the account of the prohibitions in the middle of the sūrah.

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:93
> There is no blame on those who believe and do good for what they had consumed before ˹the prohibition˺, as long as they fear Allah, have faith, and do what is good; then they believe and act virtuously, then become fully mindful ˹of Allah˺ and do righteous deeds. For Allah loves the good-doers.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Concern of the Companions That the Verse Answers**

The verse took the revealing in the response to the concern of the companions of the messenger about the Muslims who died before the revealing of the prohibition of the intoxicants in the two verses of the four things, and the concern is the act that the companions did in the form of the questioning of the state of the ones who died. The verses of the prohibition of the intoxicants took the revealing in the late period of the city of the refuge, well after the two battles of the valley and the mountain, and the timing is the act that the account of the time does in the form of the statement of the state of the community at the time of the revealing, since the many of the good Muslims who drank the intoxicants through their lives had already passed from this world at the time of the revealing of the prohibition, and the passing of the many before the prohibition is the state that the concern of the companions took in the form of the questioning of the state of the ones who died. The verse assures the companions that the fellow Muslims who lived in the obedience and the virtue will not be the blamed for the thing that they ate or consumed before the prohibition of the substances, and the assuring is the act that the verse does in the form of the statement of the no fault for the thing that was before the prohibition.

**The Similar Exemptions in the Book**

The Book keeps the similar exemption for the acts that were committed before their prohibition in the two verses of the sūrah of the family. The verse of the usury allows the ones who practiced the usury before its prohibition to keep what they had gained by it in the past, and the allowing is the act that the verse does in the form of the statement of the state of the one who refrained after receiving the warning of the Lord, since the refraining after the warning is the state that the verse keeps for the one who is not the one who persists in the usury after the prohibition. The verse of the change of the direction of the prayer states, after the statement of the change, that God would not let the belief of the community be in vain, and the stating is the act that the verse does in the form of the response to the concern of the companions about the believers who died before the change of the direction, and the response is the act that the verse does in the form of the assurance of the state of the ones who died before the change. The two verses together are the form that the Book keeps for the exemption of the act that was before the prohibition, and the form is the form that this verse keeps for the state of the ones who drank the intoxicants before the prohibition, since the three verses together are the structure that the Book gives to the matter of the act that was before the prohibition, which is the structure of the no blame for the thing that was before the warning.

**The Reading That the Consensus Contradicts**

Some of the ones who adduced the verse took it in the form of the argument that the consuming of the intoxicants is the permissible as long as the one can do it without the negative results of the enmity and the hatred and the neglect of the prayer and the remembrance of God, and the adducing is the act that the ones do in the form of the reading of the verse in the matter of the condition of the prohibition. The other of the adducing takes the verse in the form of the statement that the thing that the one consumes does not affect the one who is in the state of the fear of God and the belief and the doing of the righteous deeds and the goodness, and the stating is the act that the reading does in the form of the meaning of the verse. The conclusion of the two adducing is strongly contradicted by the practice of the messenger and the consensus of the community regarding the absolute nature of the prohibition of the intoxicants in the two verses of the four things, and the contradicting is the act that the practice and the consensus do in the form of the objection to the two readings, since the practice of the messenger and the consensus of the community are the things that the reading does not stand against. The widely accepted interpretation of the verse is the interpretation that relates the verse specifically to the ones who drank the intoxicants but died before the prohibition, and the interpretation is the one that the verse does in the form of the statement of the no fault for the thing that was before the prohibition.

**The Three Marks That the Verse Combines**

The verse combines the three things with the fear of God in the form of the belief and the doing of the righteous deeds and the goodness, and the three things are the things that are essentially identical to the three major components of the religion of the sūrah as identified in the report of the angel who came to the community with the question of the religion. The first of the components is the proper belief, and the second of the components is the submission to the obligatory acts of the worship, which is what the most of the commentators understand by the righteous deeds in this verse, and the third of the components is the goodness, which is the worshipping of God in the form of the one who sees Him. Some of the commentators describe the righteous deeds in the verse as the observance of all of the obligatory religious acts and the prohibitions, and the goodness as the performance of the acts of the worship that go beyond the obligation by which the one seeks to draw near to God, and the describing is the act that the two commentators do in the form of the reading of the three things. The description of the commentators echoes the report of the sacred tradition that states that the servant brings nothing to God that God likes more than the duties that God made incumbent upon the servant, and that the servant continues to draw near to God through the acts that go beyond the obligation until God loves the servant, and the echoing is the act that the report does in the form of the support of the reading of the three things.

**The Love of God for the Good and the Threefold Fear**

The verse closes with the statement that God loves the ones who do the good, and the statement is the statement that the Book states in the several verses in the same form. The verses that carry the statement are the verse of the sūrah of the family that states that God loves the good-doers in the command of the spending in the cause of God, and the verse of the sūrah of the family that states that God loves the good-doers in the description of the ones who give in the prosperity and the adversity and control the anger and pardon the others, and the verse of this sūrah that states that God loves the good-doers in the account of the covenant of the community of the law, and the stating of the love is the act that the Book does in the form of the repetition of the statement of the love of God for the ones who do the good, since the ones who do the good are the ones who seek to draw near to God through the acts that go beyond the obligation, and the drawing near is the act that the state of the goodness does in the form of the state of the heart of the one who is in the goodness. Some of the commentators state that the earning of the love of God in this way is the highest of the spiritual degrees and the noblest of the stations, and the stating is the act that the commentator does in the form of the naming of the state of the one who is loved by God. The other of the interpretations of the threefold mention of the fear of God in the verse is the interpretation that takes the threefold mention in the form of the emphasis, and the interpretation that takes the threefold mention in the reference to the being mindful of what was prohibited in the past and what is prohibited at the present and what will be prohibited in the future, and the interpretation that takes the threefold mention in the meaning of the having of the reverential fear of the disbelief and the major sins and the minor sins, and the three interpretations together are the form that the commentators keep for the state of the threefold mention of the fear of God in the verse.

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:94
> O believers! Allah will surely test you with game within the reach of your hands and spears to distinguish those who fear Him in secret. Whoever transgresses from now on will suffer a painful punishment.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Trial of the Game in the State of the Pilgrimage**

The verse states the trial of God for the community in the form of the game that the hands and the spears of the community obtain, and the trial is the act that God does in the form of the putting of the community in the state of the facing of the game in the state of the pilgrim sanctity. The state of the pilgrim sanctity is the state in which the one is prohibited from the hunting or the slaughtering of the animals, and the prohibition is the act that the state of the pilgrim sanctity does in the form of the forbidding of the taking of the life of the game, and the forbidding of the taking of the life of the game is the act that the state of the pilgrim sanctity does in the form of the command of the not killing of the game in the state. The trial of the verse is the trial that the pilgrims observe the prohibition in the full form, and the observing is the act that the two commentators name as the form of the trial, since the trial is the trial of the observing of the prohibition in the full form without the taking of the exception of the small or the helpless game.

**The Fear of God in the Unseen**

The verse states the purpose of the trial in the form of the knowing of God for the one who fears Him while unseen, and the knowing is the act that God does in the form of the seeing of the state of the one who is in the fear of God in the state of the unseen. The trial puts the pilgrim in the state of the facing of the game that can be captured in the easy form and has no way of the escape, which is the state of the small birds and their eggs, and the state of the game that is in the easy form of the capturing is the state that the trial does in the form of the putting of the pilgrim in the state of the temptation of the taking of the game, since the temptation of the taking of the game that is the easy to capture is the act that the trial does in the form of the test of the state of the pilgrim. The observing of the prohibition even in the state of the being out of the sight of the others is the act that the two commentators name as the form of the trial, and the observing in the unseen is the act that the pilgrim does in the form of the showing of the fear of God while unseen, since the showing of the fear of God while unseen is the act that the state of the pilgrim does in the form of the demonstration of the state of the heart of the one who is in the fear of God in the state where the eye of the people does not see.

**The Two Forms of the Game**

The verse names the two forms of the game in the form of the game that the hands obtain and the game that the spears obtain, and the two forms are the forms that the two commentators keep for the game of the trial. The game that the hands obtain is the game that is the small in the form of the birds and the small animals that the hand of the one can capture in the act of the taking, and the taking by the hand is the act that the form of the small game takes in the state of the capturing without the weapon. The game that the spears obtain is the game that is the large in the form of the large animals that the spear of the one takes in the act of the striking, and the striking with the spear is the act that the form of the large game takes in the state of the hunting with the weapon, and the two forms together are the form that the verse keeps for the game of the trial in the state of the pilgrim sanctity, since the two forms of the game are the forms that the trial of the verse does in the form of the putting of the community in the state of the facing of the game in the two forms of the small and the large.

**The Transgression After the Trial**

The verse states the charge of the one who transgresses after the trial in the form of the painful punishment, and the charge is the act that the verse does in the form of the naming of the state of the one who does not take the trial in the form of the act of the fear of God. The transgression after the trial is the act of the one who takes the game in the state of the pilgrim sanctity after the statement of the trial of the verse, and the taking of the game is the act that the verse charges in the form of the passing of the limit of the prohibition in the state of the pilgrim sanctity, since the passing of the limit is the act that the state of the one who transgresses does in the form of the not observing of the prohibition in the state of the trial. The painful punishment is the punishment that the verse states for the one who transgresses in the form of the naming of the state of the next life of the one who is in the transgression, and the naming of the state is the act that the verse does in the form of the warning of the community against the state of the transgression after the trial.

**The Verse in the Account of the Game**

The sūrah keeps this verse in the place where it opens the account of the game in the state of the pilgrim sanctity, and the opening is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the placing of the verse of the trial before the verse of the prohibition and the expiation and the verse of the lawful and the forbidden of the two kinds of the game. The account of the game runs from the verse of this trial to the verse of the prohibition of the slaughtering of the game and the expiation of the one who slaughters and the verse of the lawfulness of the game of the sea and the forbidding of the game of the land in the state of the pilgrim sanctity, and the running of the account is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the telling of the state of the community in the matter of the game in the state of the pilgrim sanctity, since the account of the game is the account that the sūrah keeps in the middle of the sūrah after the account of the prohibitions and before the account of the table that was sent down, and the account of the game is the account that the sūrah does in the form of the three verses of the trial and the prohibition and the expiation and the lawfulness and the forbidding of the two kinds of the game.

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:95
> O believers! Do not kill game while on pilgrimage. Whoever kills game intentionally must compensate by offering its equivalence—as judged by two just men among you—to be offered at the Sacred House, or by feeding the needy, or by fasting so that they may taste the consequences of their violations. Allah has forgiven what has been done. But those who persist will be punished by Allah. And Allah is Almighty, capable of punishment.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Prohibition in the State of the Sacred Pilgrimage**

The verse states the prohibition of the killing of the game in the state of the sacred pilgrimage in the form of the command of the not killing, and the command is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the address of the community in the state of the pilgrim sanctity. The prohibition is understood as the application to the ones who make the major pilgrimage or the minor pilgrimage, and the application is the act that the reading does in the form of the extension of the prohibition to the two kinds of the pilgrimage, since the state of the pilgrim sanctity is the state that the two kinds of the pilgrimage take in the form of the state of the one who is in the sacred state. The prohibition is the prohibition of the actual taking of the life of the living animal, and the taking of the life is the act that the prohibition forbids in the form of the naming of the act that is the forbidden, and the naming of the act is the act that the verse does in the form of the statement of the measure of the prohibition, since the measure of the prohibition is the measure of the taking of the life of the living animal and not the measure of the eating of the meat of the game. If the other pilgrims eat from the killed game, the repentance or the expiation is not the incumbent upon them, and the not being of the incumbency is the act that the commentator states in the form of the meaning of the prohibition, since the prohibition is the prohibition of the killing and not the prohibition of the eating of the meat of the game that the one did not kill. The one is permitted to kill the animal in the state of the pilgrim sanctity in the form that is not the form of the food, which is the form of the animal that is the immediately threatening to the human life, such as the lion or the wolf or the scorpion, and the permitting is the act that the reading states in the form of the exception of the killing of the threatening animal.

**The Occasion the Report Keeps**

The report states that the verse took the revealing after one of the helpers of the city made the minor pilgrimage in the year of the treaty and killed the wild donkey in the state of the pilgrimage, and the report is the act that the account keeps in the form of the statement of the occasion of the verse, since the occasion of the killing of the wild donkey is the occasion that the account does in the form of the support of the statement of the prohibition of the killing of the game in the state of the pilgrim sanctity. The verse establishes that the expiation is the required from the one who violates the prohibition in the intentional form, and the intentional form is the form that is not the form of the accident or the form of the forgetting of the state of the pilgrim sanctity, and the two forms are the forms that the verse does not take in the form of the expiation, since the expiation is the expiation of the one who kills in the intentional form and not the expiation of the one who kills in the form of the accident or the forgetting.

**The Several Modes of the Expiation**

The verse lists the several means of the expiation in the form of the offering of the cattle and the feeding of the poor and the fasting, and the three means are the means that the verse keeps for the one who kills the game in the intentional form. The feeding of the poor and the fasting are the means of the expiation that the verse of the oaths of this sūrah names for the broken oaths, and the naming is the act that the verse of the oaths does in the form of the parallel of the means of the expiation, since the parallel of the means of the expiation is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the repetition of the forms of the expiation in the two accounts of the oaths and the game. The primary of the means of the expiation is the giving of the equivalent of the animal that was killed as the offering that is brought to the house of the city of the birth, and the bringing is the act that the expiation does in the form of the putting of the offering in the sanctuary of the city to be distributed among the needy there, and the distributing is the act that the offering does in the form of the giving of the meat of the offering to the needy of the city. The two just men determine the worth or the equivalent of the animal that was killed, and the determining is the act that the two just men do in the form of the judging of the value of the animal, and the judging of the value is the act that the verse does in the form of the putting of the two just men in the state of the determining of the measure of the expiation. The estimation of the two just men is also the basis of the extent of the other modes of the expiation, and the basis is the act that the estimation does in the form of the putting of the amount of the feeding of the poor or the number of the days of the fasting in the measure of the standard amount of the food that the animal was determined to have contained, since the standard amount of the food is the measure that the reading of the commentator states in the form of the unit of the estimation.

**The Sanctity of the Two Cities**

The sanctity of the city of the birth is the sanctity that the verse states in the form of the forbidding of the hunting of any kind within the precincts of the city, even for the ones who are not in the state of the pilgrim sanctity, and the forbidding is the act that the sanctity does in the form of the protection of the game of the city from the hunting of the ones who are not in the state of the pilgrim sanctity. The trees of the city may also not be cut in the form of the protection of the vegetation of the city, and the not cutting is the act that the sanctity does in the form of the forbidding of the taking of the trees of the city. Some of the reports and the reports of the traditions apply the prohibitions of the hunting and the cutting of the trees to the city of the refuge as well, and the applying is the act that the reports do in the form of the extension of the sanctity to the city of the refuge. No expiation is the required for the violating of the principles of the sanctity of the two cities, and the not being of the requirement is the act that the commentator states in the form of the meaning of the verse, since the matter of the expiation in the verse relates only to the ones in the state of the pilgrim sanctity, and the relating of the expiation to the state of the pilgrim sanctity is the act that the verse does in the form of the limiting of the expiation to the one who is in the sacred state. The statement that God has forgiven what is past is the statement that the commentator takes in the reference to the illicit killing of the game that was done in the period before the religion of the sūrah, and the taking is the act that the commentator does in the form of the reading of the statement of the forgiveness in the matter of the past of the community.

**The Closing of the Relapse**

The verse closes with the charge of the one who relapses in the form of the taking of the vengeance of God upon the one, and the charge is the act that the verse does in the form of the naming of the state of the one who returns to the killing of the game in the state of the pilgrim sanctity after the forgiveness of the past. The name of God that the verse states at the close is the name of the almighty and the severe in the retribution, and the name is the name that the Book states in the several verses in the same form, and the stating is the act that the sūrah of the family and the sūrah of the story of the one who claimed the lordship and the sūrah of the story of the night do in the form of the repetition of the name, since the name of the almighty and the severe in the retribution is the name that the Book keeps for the close of the charge of the state of the one who relapses in the act that God forgave in the form of the past.

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:96
> It is lawful for you to hunt and eat seafood, as a provision for you and for travellers. But hunting on land is forbidden to you while on pilgrimage. Be mindful of Allah to Whom you all will be gathered.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Lawfulness of the Creatures of the Water**

The verse puts the creatures of the water in the state of the lawful for the community in the form of the eating that the community does under all of the circumstances, and the putting is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the naming of the state of the creatures of the water. The meat that is the lawful is the meat of the fish of the two kinds of the water, which are the fish of the fresh water and the fish of the salt water, and the two kinds together are the form that the two commentators keep for the measure of the lawfulness. The creatures that the reading excludes from the lawfulness are the creatures of the two forms, which are the form of the fish that takes the other fish in the act of the prey, such as the creature of the sea that is the swift in the water, and the form of the creature that lives in the water and on the land, such as the creature of the swamp, and the excluding is the act that the reading does in the form of the naming of the state of the two forms, since the school of the law of the one of the companions allows the creature of the swamp and the others do not allow it. The school of the law of the one party of the community takes the condition of the scales as the condition of the lawfulness of the fish, and allows the creature of the small shell, whereas the certain interpretations of the school of the law of the first of the companions put the creatures of the shell in the state of the prohibited, and the three together are the form that the schools keep for the measure of the lawfulness of the creatures of the water. For the one who is in the state of the pilgrim sanctity, the most hold that the meat is to come from the creatures that keep their life in the water only, and the holding is the act that the three commentators do in the form of the reading, since the state of the creatures that keep their life in the water only is the state that takes the creatures out of the form of the creature that can also keep its life on the land.

**The Two Words in the Readings of the Commentators**

The verse keeps the two words in the form of the game that is taken from the water and the food of the water, and the two words are the words that the commentators take in the two sets of the readings. The first of the words is the word of the game that is taken by the effort of the human being in the water, and the taking by the effort is the act that the word names in the form of the state of the creature that the one seizes in the act of the hunting in the water. The second of the words is taken by many in the form of the creatures of the water that the water puts on the shore in the state of the death, and the creatures in this state can be gathered and eaten by the one who finds them, and the putting on the shore is the act that the water does in the form of the state of the creatures without the act of the one. The most accept the eating of the creatures in this state as the exception from the prohibition of the dead thing that the several verses of the Book forbid, and the accepting is the act that the most do in the form of the reading of the exception, since the reading of the exception rests on the state of the water that the report of the tradition names as the pure in the form that the one can use it for the purifications of the worship. The commentators of the one party of the community take the opposite reading, and hold that the eating of the creatures that the water puts on the shore in the state of the death is the forbidden in every case, and the holding is the act that the two commentators do in the form of the reading of the two words, and their reading is the reading that the first of the words refers to the creatures that are seized fresh in the state of the pilgrim sanctity, and the second of the words refers to the creatures that were seized before and kept by the salt as the provision of the journey. The other set of the readings takes the two words of the provision in the verse, and the first of the words points at the abundance of the fresh creatures for the ones who have their dwelling near the water or who move along it, and the second of the words points at the keeping of the creatures by the salt for the use of the ones who travel the long roads on the land, and the two together are the form that the two commentators keep for the two words of the provision.

**The Forbidding of the Creatures of the Land**

The verse puts the creatures of the land in the state of the forbidden for the one who is in the making of the pilgrimage in the form of the general or the absolute sense, and the putting is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the naming of the state of the creatures of the land while the one is in the sacred state. The verse of the prohibition before is the verse that names the not killing of the creatures of the land in the state of the pilgrim sanctity as the act that is the forbidden, and this verse is the verse that names the eating from the creatures of the land in the state of the pilgrimage in the form of the forbidden, whether the act of the killing is the act of the one himself or the act of the other, and the two verses together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the matter of the creatures of the land in the sacred state, since the early authorities took the two verses in the two readings, which are the reading that the killing only is the forbidden for the one who is in the pilgrimage, and the reading that the one who is in the pilgrimage is to keep himself from the eating of the meat of the hunted creatures of the land in the whole, and the meat includes the meat that the one killed by the own hand and the meat that the other killed.

**The Positions That the Accounts Keep**

The cousin of the messenger and the son-in-law of the messenger took the eating of the meat of the hunted creatures of the land of any kind as the thing that is the not good for the one who is in the pilgrimage, and the taking is the act that the one did in the form of the reading of the state of the eating. The second and the third of the ones who took over the command after the messenger took the eating of the meat of the creatures that the others killed as the lawful, and the taking is the act that the two did in the form of the reading of the state of the eating of the meat of the creatures that the others killed. The reports keep the statement that the messenger received the meat of the hunted creatures of the land as the gift in the state of the pilgrim sanctity and that the messenger put the meat back, and the putting back is the act that the reports name in the form of the state of the messenger, although the putting back may be because the messenger held that the meat was the meat that the one hunted and killed on behalf of the messenger in the form of the giving of the gift, and the report of the tradition states that the meat of the hunted creatures of the land is the lawful for the one who is in the pilgrimage to take as long as the one did not do the hunting of it and did not put the hunting of it on the one who hunts for him. The three schools of the law accept that the one who is in the pilgrimage can take the meat of the hunted creatures of the land as long as the killing of the creature was not the killing of the one himself or the killing that was done on behalf of the one, and the school of the law of the first of the companions takes the verse of the prohibition before in the literal form, and holds that the forbidding reaches only the creatures of the land that the one hunted and killed by the own hands, while the meat of the creatures that were hunted on behalf of the one is the lawful for the one to take. The opinion that stands against this holds that the general forbidding of the hunted creatures of the land in this verse puts the meat of every of the hunted creatures of the land in the state of the forbidden for the one who is in the pilgrimage, and the holders of this opinion are the cousin of the messenger and the early commentator and a number of the prominent authorities of the early time, and the school of the law of the one party of the community holds the same opinion. Some of the authorities of the early time put the eating of the meat of the creatures that the one hunted and killed before the entering of the one into the state of the pilgrim sanctity in the state of the possible, although the others, and among them the early commentator, do not take this distinction, and the commentators of the one party of the community also do not take it.

**The Closing of the Verse**

The verse closes with the command of the revering of God in the form of the statement of the gathering of the community unto God, and the revering is the act that the command puts in the form of the duty of the community in the matter of the creatures of the land in the state of the pilgrim sanctity. The statement of the gathering unto God is the statement that the sūrah keeps for the close of the verse in the form of the naming of the state of the community in the next life, and the naming of the state is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the putting of the community in the state of the awareness of the gathering, since the awareness of the gathering unto God is the act that the close of the verse does in the form of the invitation of the community to the state of the revering of God in the matter of the lawfulness and the forbidding of the two kinds of the game in the state of the pilgrim sanctity.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:97
> Allah has made the Ka’bah—the Sacred House—a sanctuary of wellbeing for all people, along with the sacred months, the sacrificial animals, and the ˹offerings decorated with˺ garlands. All this so you may know that Allah knows whatever is in the heavens and whatever is on the earth, and that He has ˹perfect˺ knowledge of everything.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The House as the Support for the People**

The verse states the house of the city of the birth in the form of the support for the people, and the support is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the naming of the function of the house in the matter of the people. Although the rites of the pilgrimage and the entering of the sanctuary of the city and of the house were put in the state of the exclusive of the community of the sūrah in the final year of the life of the messenger, and the ones who are not of the community are not the ones who can enter the sanctuary after that, the present verse is the verse that stands with the verses that put the sanctuary of the city in the state of the significance that reaches all of the people, and the putting is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the description of the house as the support. The verses that carry the similar statements of the good that the sanctuary and the rites of the sanctuary do for the people are the verse of the sūrah of the family that makes the sacred house the centre and the sanctuary for the people, and the verse of the sūrah of the family that names the first house of the worship established for the people as the blessed sanctuary and the guide for the people, and the verse of the sūrah of the story of the elephant that appoints the sacred mosque for all of the people, the residents and the visitors, and the three verses together are the form that the Book keeps for the statement of the significance of the sanctuary for the people.

**The Support in the Readings of the Commentators**

The word of the people is the word that is not necessarily the universal in the sense, and it can mean simply the people of the land, and the sense is the act that the commentators take in the form of the reading of the measure of the word. Some of the commentators explain that the house is described as the support for the people because the house kept the affairs of the people of the land in the state of the order in the period before the religion of the sūrah, and the keeping of the order is the act that the house did in the form of the regulation of the affairs of the people of the land. As the city is the place of the sanctuary for the wild birds and the animals, in the form that the hunting of them is not the allowed within the borders of the city, so the city kept the human beings in the state of the sanctuary, and the serving is the act that the commentator does in the form of the parallel of the two sanctuaries. Since even the people of the idolatry observed the sacred status of the city and maintained the ban on the violence in the sanctuary of the house, the city kept the strong away from the weak, and the evil away from the virtuous, and the oppressor away from the one who is oppressed, and the keeping away is the act that the commentator does in the form of the naming of the three relations that the city regulated. Some of the commentators interpret the description of the house as the support for the people in the meaning of the support for the people of the land specifically in the period before the religion, in the time when no authority kept the order of the land, and that the house now keeps the community of the sūrah in the state of the support by the orienting of the religious rites of the pilgrimage and the prayer, and the orienting is the act that the house does in the form of the putting of the rites of the community in the direction of the house. One of the later commentators takes the description of the house as the support for the people in the indication that God made the house in such a form that the human beings have the natural awareness of the majesty of the house and the desire that is in them from the origin to move toward it, and the taking is the act that the commentator does in the form of the reading of the natural state of the human being toward the house.

**The Sacred Month and the Offerings and the Garlands**

The verse mentions the sacred month and the offerings and the garlands along with the house in the form of the support for the people, and the three together are the things that the sūrah keeps in the form of the naming of the state of the sanctuary. The sacred month is the month that the commentator takes in the reference to the month of the pilgrimage, which is the month in which the pilgrimage took the doing in the time before and in the time after, and the taking is the act that the commentator does in the form of the reading of the specific month. The other of the readings takes the sacred month in the form of the four months of the sanctity collectively, in which the travelers were the safe from the harm in the period before the religion in the land, and the four months are the months that the sūrah of this account names in the verse of the command and the prohibition, and the naming is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the reference to the months. The offerings are the sacrificial animals that are destined for the sanctuary and that cannot be harmed, and the state of the offerings is the state that the verse names in the form of the protection of the animals from the harming. The garlands are the ornamental wreaths that the people of the period before the religion carried on the necks of the sacrificial animals, and the carrying of the garlands is the act that the people did in the form of the showing of the status of the animals to the others, in order that the others would not harm the animals or impede them in the state where the animals were being led to the house, and the showing of the status is the act that the garlands did in the form of the sign of the offering.

**The Purpose of the Knowledge**

The verse states the purpose of the support in the form of the knowledge, and the knowledge is the knowledge that the community is to take in the matter of the house and the sacred month and the offerings and the garlands. The purpose is the purpose of the knowing that God knows the thing that is in the heavens and the thing that is on the earth, and the knowing of God for the two is the act that the sūrah states in the form of the statement of the state of God in the matter of the support, since the state of God is the state of the one who knows the thing that is in the heavens and the thing that is on the earth, and the knowing of the two is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the naming of the state of God as the knower of all of the things. The sūrah keeps the statement of the knowledge at the close of the verse, and the keeping is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the putting of the knowledge after the naming of the four things that are the support, since the putting of the knowledge after the naming is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the statement of the ground of the support, which is the ground of the knowledge of God for the thing that is in the heavens and the thing that is on the earth.

**The Verse in the Account of the Sanctity**

The sūrah keeps this verse in the place where it states the account of the sanctity of the city of the birth after the account of the game in the state of the pilgrim sanctity, and the stating is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the placing of the verse of the support at the end of the account of the game. The account of the game runs from the verse of the trial to the verse of the lawfulness of the creatures of the water and the forbidding of the creatures of the land, and this verse is the verse that states the support of the house and the sacred month and the offerings and the garlands for the people, and the two accounts together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the matter of the sanctuary of the city, which is the form of the account of the game in the state of the pilgrim sanctity and the account of the support of the house for the people held together in the two accounts, and the two accounts together are the structure that the sūrah gives to the matter of the sanctity of the city of the birth in the middle of the sūrah.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:98
> Know that Allah is severe in punishment and that He is All-Forgiving, Most Merciful.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Severity at the Close of the Prohibitions**

The verse states the description of God in the form of the severity in the retribution at the close of the series of the prohibitions that relate to the pilgrimage, and the severity is the state that the verse names for God in the form of the act of the taking of the retribution from the one who passes the limits of the prohibitions. The same phrase is found at the end of the second verse of this sūrah, and the second verse similarly contains the commands and the prohibitions that relate to the pilgrimage, and the finding of the same phrase is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the parallel of the two closes, since the parallel of the two closes is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the keeping of the same form of the statement of the severity at the end of the two accounts of the pilgrimage. The Book states the same description in the several verses of the other sūrahs, and the stating is the act that the sūrah of the family and the sūrah of the story of the story and the sūrah of the criterion do in the form of the repetition of the phrase, since the phrase of the severity in the retribution is the phrase that the Book keeps for the close of the charge of the state of the one who is in the defiance of God or the transgression of the limits of God.

**The Pairing of the Two Sets of the Qualities**

The verse pairs the description of the severity in the retribution with the description of the forgiveness and the mercy, and the pairing is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the holding of the two sets of the qualities of God together in the one verse. The pairing of the severity and the mercy is the pairing that the Book keeps in the similar form in the verse of the sūrah of the thunder and the verse of the sūrah of the story of the story, and the keeping is the act that the two sūrahs do in the form of the repetition of the pairing. The pairing reflects the principle that is widely held, which is the principle that the true believer is always in the state of the being suspended between the fear of the wrath of God and the hope in the mercy of God, and the suspension is the act that the principle does in the form of the naming of the state of the heart of the one who is in the belief. Some of the commentators quote the saying that if the fear of the believer were weighed against the hope of the believer, the two would be the equal, and the quoting is the act that the commentator does in the form of the support of the principle of the suspension of the heart of the believer between the two.

**The Mercy That Takes the Precedence**

The fact that the verse concludes with the mention of the qualities of the mercy and the forgiveness is understood by some in the meaning that these qualities take the precedence in the nature of God, and the understanding is the act that the commentator does in the form of the reading of the order of the qualities in the verse, since the order of the qualities in the verse is the order that puts the mercy and the forgiveness after the severity, and the putting of the mercy at the end is the act that the reading does in the form of the statement of the precedence of the mercy. The sacred report of the tradition that states that the mercy of God takes the precedence over the wrath of God is the report that the commentators keep in the form of the support of the reading, and the support is the act that the report does in the form of the naming of the state of the mercy of God in the form of the thing that comes before the wrath of God, and the naming of the state is the act that the report does in the form of the statement of the precedence of the mercy in the nature of God.

**The Verse as the Close of the Account of the Pilgrimage**

The sūrah keeps this verse in the place where it closes the account of the pilgrimage and the game and the sanctity of the city, and the closing is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the placing of the verse of the two sets of the qualities at the end of the account. The account of the pilgrimage runs from the verse of the trial of the game to the verse of the support of the house, and this verse is the verse that closes the account in the form of the statement of the two sets of the qualities of God, and the closing of the account is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the putting of the community in the state of the awareness of the two sets of the qualities at the end of the account of the prohibitions of the pilgrimage, since the awareness of the two sets of the qualities is the act that the close of the account does in the form of the putting of the community in the state of the fear and the hope in the matter of the obeying of the prohibitions of the account.

**The Two Statements in the Form of the Close**

The verse keeps the two statements in the form of the close, and the two statements are the statement of the severity in the retribution and the statement of the forgiveness and the mercy. The first of the statements is the statement that the sūrah does in the form of the charge of the state of the one who passes the limits of the prohibitions of the account, and the second of the statements is the statement that the sūrah does in the form of the invitation of the community to the state of the hope in the mercy of God, and the two statements together are the form that the sūrah keeps for the close of the account of the pilgrimage, which is the form of the charge of the severity and the invitation of the mercy held together in the one verse, and the two together are the act that the sūrah does in the form of the putting of the community in the state of the suspension between the two at the end of the account of the prohibitions of the pilgrimage in the middle of the sūrah.

---

## Sūrah al-Māʾidah 5:99
> The Messenger’s duty is only to deliver ˹the message˺. And Allah ˹fully˺ knows what you reveal and what you conceal.

**Expanded Commentary**

**The Proclamation as the Only Duty**

The verse states the duty of the messenger in the form of the proclamation only, and the proclamation is the act that the verse names as the only duty of the messenger in the matter of the message. The verse of the command of the obeying in the account of the prohibitions of this sūrah also carries the reminder of the messenger that his only responsibility is the delivering of the clear proclamation, and the carrying is the act that the verse of the command does in the form of the parallel of the statement of this verse, since the parallel of the two statements is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the repetition of the statement of the duty of the messenger in the two places of the sūrah, and the repetition of the statement of the duty is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the putting of the community in the state of the awareness of the state of the messenger in the matter of the message.

**The Knowledge of God for the Open and the Hidden**

The verse states the knowledge of God for the thing that the community makes open and the thing that the community conceals, and the knowledge is the act that the sūrah does in the form of the naming of the state of God in the matter of the two acts of the community. The knowledge of God for the unseen is the knowledge that the Book mentions in the different ways in the several verses of the other sūrahs, and the mentioning is the act that the Book does in the form of the repetition of the statement of the knowledge of God. The knowledge of God for the thing that the human beings make open and the thing that they conceal of their own thoughts and their own nature is the knowledge that the Book states specifically in the verse of the sūrah of the family where God tells the first of the people that He knows the secrets of the heavens and the earth and what the people reveal and what they conceal, and the verse of the sūrah of the light that states the knowledge of God for the thing that the community enters in the public places, and the verse of the sūrah of the story of the youth that names God as the one who brings forth what is hidden in the heavens and the earth and knows what the people conceal and what they reveal, and the verse of the sūrah of the criterion that states the knowledge of God for the thing that is in the heavens and the earth and what the people conceal and what they reveal and what is hidden in the heart, and the stating of the knowledge is the act that the Book does in the form of the repetition of the statement of the knowledge of God for the open and the hidden of the community.
